## Document 037: 20261027【投研報告】市場快訊_MSCI降評風險升高，印尼股市保守看待

MSCI 降評風險升高，印尼股市保守看待
2026 年 10 月 27 日
一、 市場動態
1. MSCI 擬將印尼降至獨立市場，降評風險明顯升高：MSCI 於 10 月 15 日宣布，啟動
將印尼股市由「新興市場（Emerging Market）」直接降至「獨立市場（Standalone
Market） 」的諮詢程序，意見徵詢將於2027 年 4 月 30 日截止，最晚於 2027 年 6 月
30 日公布最終決定 。MSCI 原先於今年 1 月提出可能降至 「邊境市場 （Frontier Market
） 」 ，此次則跳過「邊境市場」提出直接將該國股市降至「獨立市場」的方案，顯示對

印尼市場治理的疑慮進一步加深。雖然「邊境市場」仍可能是替代分類方案，但MSCI
強調，除非有明確證據顯示市場治理及運作出現實質改善，否則預期將依目前提案重
新分類，顯示此次降評風險較過去顯著升高。
2. 股權透明度與交易秩序問題為 MSCI 主要疑慮：MSCI 指出，印尼股市長期存在股權
結構不透明、實際自由流通股比例難以確認、疑似協調交易，以及市場監理執法成效
不足等問題，影響國際投資人對市場公平性與可投資性的信心。雖然印尼主管機關已
提出強化持股超過 1%股東資訊揭露、建立高持股集中度（HSC）監理機制，以及規
劃逐步將最低自由流通股比例提高至 15%等改革措施，但MSCI 認為相關改革尚未展
現持續且具實質性的改善成果。由於問題涉及市場制度與治理結構，改善所需時間可
能較一般短期市場波動更長。
3. 股市反彈後再度回落，外資撤離壓力仍高：受降評消息衝擊，印尼雅加達綜合指數 10
月 16 日重挫 11.57%，創 1998 年以來最大單日跌幅，19 日再跌 3.64%，盤中最低
觸及 4,907.37 點 。 雖然20 日至 23 日連續四個交易日反彈 ， 但26 日再度下跌 1.22%

，收於 5,373.09 點，各產業類股全面收黑，顯示市場信心仍不穩定。根據 Reuters
報導，截至 10 月 19 日，2026 年外資已累計淨賣超印尼股票約 48.8 億美元。此外，
印尼證交所 26 日宣布，將引進獨立專家及外國投資人代表參與高持股集中度審查，
並自 11 月起加強調查疑似協調交易，必要時凍結可疑帳戶，以回應 MSCI 對市場制
度的疑慮。
二、 下跌原因分析：
1. 股市：潛在指數剔除與資金外流，持續壓抑市場評價
若印尼股市最終降至「獨立市場」， 原則上將不再納入MSCI 新興市場及邊境市場相關
指數，追蹤相關指數的被動式基金將須依規則調整持股，實際處理方式仍以 MSCI 後
續公告為準。若改降至「邊境市場」，則可能獲得部分邊境市場資金承接，衝擊相對較
小。Reuters 引述多家金融機構估計，印尼股市降至獨立市場可能引發約 30 億至 60

策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
億美元被動資金流出，其中 UBS 估計約 40 億美元，並指出主動型基金另有約 150 億
美元潛在撤資風險，惟實際規模仍視最終分類結果及基金策略而定。
2. 匯率：外資撤離增加印尼盾貶值壓力
印尼股市的降評風險不僅衝擊股市，也可能透過外資撤離增加印尼盾貶值壓力。印尼
盾 10 月 16 日盤中一度貶至 18,070 兌 1 美元，為 7 月下旬以來最弱水準之一，當日
收盤約 18,000。若後續國際資金持續流出，可能進一步增加股匯市波動，對以外幣計
價、且未完全避險的印尼基金及 ETF 形成額外壓力。雖然印尼央行可透過匯市干預及

流動性管理措施穩定金融市場，但在資金外流壓力尚未緩解前，印尼盾仍可能維持偏
弱格局。
3. 基金與 ETF：指數權重下滑，單一市場曝險風險升高
MSCI 相關指數調整已對印尼股票產生實質影響。Reuters 引用 LSEG 資料顯示，印尼
在 MSCI 新興市場指數的權重已由 2026 年初的 1.16%降至 9 月底的 0.43%，相關成
分股亦由年初 18 檔減至 9 檔，反映部分指數資金調整壓力已提前出現。對印尼單一
國家基金及 ETF 而言，主要風險來自股市下跌、資金流出及印尼盾波動；主動式基金
雖可透過選股降低部分影響，但仍難以完全迴避整體市場風險。不同產品實際受到的
影響，仍應依追蹤指數、主要持股及匯率避險安排個別評估。
三、 市場看法：
1. 制度性問題難以速解，降評風險將持續牽動市場：我們認為，此次 MSCI 降評風險並
非單純反映經濟成長放緩或企業獲利不佳，而是涉及市場透明度、交易公平性與監理
執法等結構性問題。尤其 MSCI 不僅啟動降評諮詢，更持續限制印尼股票的部分指數

調整，包括不提高外資納入因子、不新增相關成分股，以及不進行向上規模分類調整
，顯示市場可投資性疑慮尚未獲得有效解決。儘管政府試圖推動改革，但制度修正仍
須經過執行與成效驗證，才能重新取得國際投資人的信任，因此短期內降評風險仍難
完全消除。
2. 外資撤離與指數權重下降，市場信心修復恐需較長時間：印尼今年以來外資已大幅賣
超，MSCI 新興市場指數中的權重亦明顯下降，顯示國際投資人對當地市場的疑慮已
逐漸反映於實際資金流向。我們認為，即使印尼最終成功避免降評，部分國際機構投
資人仍可能因市場透明度、流動性及治理品質疑慮而維持較低曝險。此外，MSCI 對
部分指數調整的凍結措施尚未解除，短期內新增指數資金流入機會仍受到限制，預期
國際資金回流與市場評價修復均可能需要較長時間。
3. 監理改革力度加大，但尚不足以扭轉保守展望：印尼政府近期持續加強改革，包括提
高資訊透明度、強化交易監控、對違規交易開罰，以及引進外部獨立人士參與監理審
查。10 月 26 日該國政府進一步提出主動調查疑似協調交易及凍結可疑帳戶等措施，

11 月 2 日起亦規劃將高持股集中度股票排除於雅加達綜合指數之外 。 相關改革有助改

策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
善交易秩序及短期市場信心，但部分措施仍待正式實施，且實際成效尚未獲得 MSCI
認可。考量制度性問題改善需要時間，政策利多雖可能帶動短線反彈，但尚不足以改
變對印尼股市中期前景的保守判斷。
4. 股市跌深不代表利空出盡，中期風險報酬吸引力偏低：印尼股市在 10 月 16 日及 19
日大幅修正後，雖曾連續四個交易日反彈，但 26 日再度回落，顯示市場尚未形成穩
定回升趨勢。我們認為，當前股市面臨的不僅是短期賣壓，更包括國際投資人信任下
降及風險溢價提高，即使企業基本面未同步惡化，仍可能因評價下修而承受股價壓力

。因此，短線不排除政策利多或跌深後出現技術性反彈，但在 MSCI 態度、外資流向
與印尼盾走勢明顯改善前，反彈延續性仍有疑慮，印尼股市中期展望維持保守。
四、市場展望：
1. 印尼股市維持保守展望，單一市場風險報酬吸引力不足：考量 MSCI 降評風險尚未解除、
外資撤離壓力持續 ， 以及市場治理改革仍有待驗證 ， 我們認為印尼股市未來數季的風險報
酬吸引力相對有限。即使近期股價明顯回落，仍難認定相關風險已充分反映。相較市場制
度成熟、國際資金流動性較佳的新興市場，印尼股市可能持續面臨較高風險溢價，因此對
單一印尼市場的集中曝險應維持審慎態度。
2. 此事件對新興市場指數與東協指數影響相對有限：根據 MSCI 資料，截至2026 年 9 月底
，印尼股市在 MSCI Emerging Market 指數與 MSCI AC ASEAN 指數中的權重分別約為
0.43%與 7.55%，因此我們認為印尼潛在降評對相關指數的直接衝擊相對有限。此外，新
興市場各國經濟結構與產業組成有所差異 ， 區域型基金可透過跨國分散降低單一市場風險

。惟後續仍須留意印尼事件對整體新興市場投資信心造成的外溢影響 ， 以及全球貿易與匯
率波動風險。

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
美國服務業與內需維持擴張，通膨黏著與中東風險牽動政策前景 
經濟 
⚫ 9 月美國 ISM 服務業 PMI 由 55.4 降至 54.9，連續第 27 個月維持擴張。
商業活動及新訂單雖較前月回落 ， 仍保持穩健成長 ； 就業重返擴張區間 ，
內需與勞動市場仍具韌性 。 惟價格指數續升創高 ， 出口與進口動能轉弱 ，
後續仍須關注通膨壓力及外需變化。 
⚫ 8 月美國商品及服務貿易逆差擴大至 1,056 億美元 ， 較上月增加13.7%。
出口 3,152 億美元、進口4,208 億美元，分別月增1.4%及 4.3%。出口成
長主要受非貨幣黃金、原油及半導體帶動，進口則以原油、黃金及資本
財增加為主 。 累計前8 月貿易逆差 5,570 億美元 ， 較去年同期縮減19.9%，
惟近期進口增幅高於出口，使逆差再度擴大。 
⚫ 9 月 FOMC 會議紀要顯示，AI 投資持續帶動經濟與就業動能，勞動市
場仍具韌性；惟去通膨進展有限，核心服務通膨及成本轉嫁壓力仍存。
全體官員支持升息 1 碼，傾向維持限制性政策，並關注 AI 融資、公債
市場波動及網路安全等金融風險。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 近期美伊緊張情勢再升溫，川普改口不排除選前對伊朗採取軍事行動，
伊朗則持續尋求重啟談判；同時葉門青年運動頻繁襲擊沙國目標，加劇
中東安全風險。若衝突擴大，恐影響荷姆茲與曼德海峽能源運輸，推升
油氣價格並增添全球經濟下行風險。 
⚫ 美國啟動 337 調查，針對 AI 伺服器垂直供電系統（VPD）相關專利侵
權爭議展開審查，鴻海、台達電、廣達等約 20 家業者列名。由於涉及
AI 伺服器關鍵供電技術，若最終認定侵權，相關產品恐面臨輸美限制，
對供應鏈營運帶來影響。 
短評 
⚫ 美國服務業與內需仍具韌性，AI 投資亦支撐經濟動能，惟貿易逆差擴
大、成本壓力升高及通膨黏著，使聯準會仍需維持偏緊縮立場。加上美
伊與中東局勢升溫，能源價格與金融市場波動風險上升，後續景氣不確
定性仍高。

世銀上調經濟展望；印度央行近四年來首度升息 
經濟 
⚫ 世銀上調東亞及太平洋地區 2026 年 GDP 預估至 4.5%；印度 2026 財年

一、 美國—美國服務業與內需維持擴張，通膨黏著與中東風險牽動政策前景 
2026 年 9 月美國 ISM 服務業採購經理人指數(Services PMI) 為 54.9，較上月
55.4 下降 0.5 個百分點，已連續第 27 個月維持於 50 以上擴張區間，且高於近 12
個月平均值 54.1，服務業景氣仍持續穩健擴張。細項觀察，商業活動指數由 61.7
降至 56.5，新訂單指數由 60.9 降至 59.8，雖較前月回落，但仍維持高檔擴張；就
業指數則由 47.8 回升至 50.1，三個月來首度重返擴張區間。價格指數由 72.6 升
至 74.0，創 2022 年 7 月以來新高。供應商交貨指數由 51.3 升至 53.2，交貨時間
再度延長；庫存指數由56.7 升至 57.8，企業持續提前備貨以因應未來成本上升風
險。此外，未完成訂單指數升至 56.6，創 2022 年 7 月以來高點，惟新出口訂單
指數由 56.3 大幅降至 46.9，八個月來首度跌入收縮區間，進口指數亦由 56.3 降
至 52.9，外部需求及採購動能均降溫。整體而言，美國服務業景氣仍維持穩健擴
張，內需與就業展現一定韌性，但燃料價格上漲、關稅政策、供應鏈限制及地緣
政治等因素持續推升企業成本，後續仍須關注通膨壓力及外需變化對景氣的影響。 
2026 年 8 月美國商品及服務貿易逆差擴大至 1,056 億美元 ， 較上月修正後928
億美元增加 127 億美元，增幅 13.7%。當月出口 3,152 億美元，較上月增加 45 億
美元(+1.4%)；進口 4,208 億美元，較上月增加 172 億美元(+4.3%)。其中，商品貿
易逆差擴大至 1,366 億美元，服務貿易順差則小幅增至 310 億美元。出口成長主
要受工業用品及原材料出口增加 63 億美元帶動，其中非貨幣黃金、原油及燃油
出口增幅較為顯著，資本財出口亦受半導體、電腦及電腦周邊設備需求增加所支
撐；進口方面，工業用品及原材料進口增加 91 億美元，資本財進口增加 62 億美
元，其中原油、非貨幣黃金、半導體及工業機械進口增幅較大。累計2026 年 1 至
8 月，美國商品及服務貿易逆差為 5,570 億美元，較 2025 年同期減少 1,382 億美
元，縮減 19.9%；同期出口成長 11.8%，進口增加 4.4%，顯示美國對外貿易失衡
情況較去年同期有所改善，惟近期進口增幅明顯高於出口，使貿易逆差再度呈現
擴大趨勢。 
2026 年 9 月 FOMC 會議紀要重點如下：(1)經濟：若干官員認為美國經濟成
長動能有所增強 ，AI 投資規模與速度持續超乎預期 ， 帶動企業投資與生產力提升 ；

用相關內容。

5

企業活動則受到企業獲利維持高檔及部分政策因素支持；(2)就業：多數官員表示
勞動市場近期略有改善，就業成長維持穩健，失業率仍處低檔，整體勞動市場大
致符合充分就業狀態，供需風險大致平衡；(3)通膨：官員普遍認為近期去通膨進
展有限，非住房核心服務通膨仍偏高，人力、能源及部分進口成本轉嫁情形仍值
得關注；部分官員擔憂 AI 投資與相關支出擴張，可能推升需求並增加通膨持續
性風險；(4)貨幣政策：鑑於經濟活動持續擴張、勞動市場具韌性且通膨仍高於目
標，全體官員支持升息 1 碼。許多官員認為維持較具限制性的政策立場有助降低
通膨風險，部分官員則認為未來可能仍需進一步緊縮政策，以確保通膨回到目標
水準；(5)金融風險：官員普遍認為金融條件整體仍支持經濟成長，但部分官員關
切 AI 基礎設施快速擴張所衍生的融資風險，以及美國公債市場波動、網路安全
與其他潛在金融穩定風險。 
近期美伊及中東局勢再度升溫，市場對區域衝突擴大的疑慮升高。美國總統
川普由先前宣稱期中選舉前不會攻擊伊朗 ， 改口表示 「仍有可能」 採取軍事行動 ，
且美軍已就相關軍事選項進行準備，使美伊對抗風險再度攀升；另一方面，伊朗
雖有意透過斡旋重啟談判，但雙方在核計畫與制裁議題上仍存重大分歧。此外，
葉門青年運動持續升高對沙烏地阿拉伯攻擊，利雅德哈立德國王國際機場一週內
多次遇襲，造成多人死傷，凸顯區域安全風險升高。整體而言，若美伊衝突或代
理人戰事進一步升級，恐衝擊荷姆茲海峽及曼德海峽等能源運輸要道，推升國際
油氣價格、加劇通膨壓力，並增添全球金融市場波動及經濟成長下行風險。 
美國國際貿易委員會(USITC)於 10 月 9 日正式啟動 337 條款調查，針對特定
垂直供電系統(Vertical Power Delivery，VPD)、 相關零組件及採用該技術的運算設
備是否涉及專利侵權展開審查。此案由美國電源技術廠商 Vicor Corporation 提出
申訴，主張相關產品侵犯其專利權，並要求發布有限排除令及停止侵權命令，以
限制涉案產品進入美國市場 。 調查對象涵蓋全球約20 家企業 ， 包括台灣的鴻海 、
台達電、廣達及其美國關係企業，另有中國的立訊精密、工業富聯、德國的英飛
凌等國際大廠列名 。 本案聚焦 AI 伺服器及高效能運算領域關鍵的供電架構技術 ，
該技術有助提升晶片供電效率、降低能源損耗，已廣泛應用於資料中心與 AI 運
算設備。隨著全球 AI 基礎建設投資持續擴大，相關專利布局與授權爭議亦日益
升溫。USITC 指出，此次立案僅代表調查程序正式展開，並不表示已認定侵權，
後續將由行政法官進行聽證及審理，預計於 45 天內訂定調查時程。台灣經濟部

則表示，337 調查屬企業間智慧財產權爭議，政府將持續關注案情發展，並於必

二、歐元區—法國殖利率短期內恐難降溫 
 德國工業生產在 7 月月減 1.2%後，8 月回彈 2%，主要反映部分汽車業者在 7
月提前進行歲修 ， 並且在8 月陸續復工後 ， 帶動生產及庫存回補需求所致 ； 此外 ，
受到 8 月萊茵河水位降低，沿岸化學工廠物流運輸受阻，致化工產業產出月減
1.2%為主要拖累因素。儘管工業生產如期反彈，惟工業訂單卻大幅下跌 10.6%，
主要是與軍工產業相關的訂單如飛機、船舶、軍用車輛、武器彈藥等訂單減少，
顯示政府前期大額採購效應逐漸消退；除軍工產業外，其餘產業訂單則大致維持
穩定。 
 法國 8 月工業生產月減 0.3%，為連續第二個月下滑，主要係夏季用電高峰過
後電力產出回落，帶動能源部門生產減少所致。另方面，製造業生產月增0.3%，
其中造紙、化工及電子電機設備等產業自前期低點回升，部分抵銷汽車等運輸設
備產出月減 2.2%的拖累。不過，法國 9 月 INSEE 新訂單指標由 8 月的-13.8 點降
至-15.8 點，顯示企業接單偏弱，主要反映出口需求疲軟。此外，相較於德國近期
透過國防及基礎建設支出擴大支撐工業生產，法國財政擴張力道相對有限，使製
造業景氣較依賴外部需求，德、法兩國製造業表現因而呈現逐漸分化的趨勢。 
 展望未來，近期歐元區各國殖利率上升，除反映通膨壓力增強、升息預增加
之外，亦部分反映西班牙宣布於 11 月 29 日提前進行大選、法國財政前景不明朗
等政治因素影響。儘管較高的殖利率有助於促使法國政府推動財政整頓，但考量
相關改革涉及福利及退休制度調整等政治敏感議題，不確定性較高，短期內德國
與法國殖利率差距恐難縮小。整體而言，法國製造業在出口需求疲弱及財政支撐
相對有限下，景氣復甦力道恐仍較為溫和，預料德、法兩國經濟及工業活動表現
分化的情形短期內仍將持續。 
三、日本—薪資成長支撐內需，消費信心持穩 
 8 月薪資年增 3.8%，其中經常性薪資年增 3.8%、加班費年增 5.2%、實質薪資
年增 1.5%， 主要受惠於春鬥加薪效應延續及通膨壓力較去年趨緩所帶動 ， 有助於
進一步提升民眾購買力，支撐消費與經濟復甦。

9 月消費者信心指數小幅下滑 0.1 點至 35.4，但表現仍優於市場預期。受食品

四、中國—歐中達成貿易談判，外部風險暫時降溫 
中國 9 月外匯存底降至 3.40 兆美元，較前月減少 381 億美元，反映美元升
值、政府以外匯購買黃金儲備，加上歐美殖利率上升，造成中國持有的海外債券
資產出現評價損失，皆為外匯存底下滑的主要原因。惟亦不排除中國外匯流入情
況可能有所轉弱，例如出口成長放緩、進口需求增加，或資本外流規模高於預期
等因素。 
展望未來 ， 近期歐中談判達成16 項共識 ， 未來中國混合動力車出口至歐盟將
面臨調整壓力，但歐盟進一步升高貿易保護措施的風險已降溫。另一方面，基於
中國維持大額經常帳順差、出口商結匯需求及年底季節性資金流入支撐，預期人
民幣仍有升值空間，並帶動外匯存底回升。 
五、印度及東南亞—世銀上調經濟展望；印度央行近四年來首度升息 
(1)世界銀行 2026 年 10 月《東亞與太平洋地區經濟展望報告》指出，受人工智慧
（AI） 相關出口與投資強勁帶動，將2026 年東亞與太平洋地區 （EAP） 經濟成長
率預測上修 0.3 個百分點至 4.5%，2027 年與 2028 年將分別放緩至 4.4%與 4.3%，
其中，越南、馬來西亞、泰國 GDP 大幅上調至 7.4%、5.1%及 2.0%。世銀指出，
AI 相關商品占多數經濟體出口增長的一半以上，其中，馬來西亞、菲律賓、泰國
和越南的 AI 出口貢獻率更超過七成，但扣除 AI 相關產品後，區域貿易成長呈現
疲弱或負增長，警告該地區的經濟韌性高度依賴 AI 相關製造業和出口，使其容
易受到全球科技支出逆轉的影響，建議應將 AI 產品生產優勢轉化為廣泛應用，
以提升生產力，並創造更多優質就業機會。此外，美伊戰爭推升能源價格，各國
政府比其他地區更積極因應 ， 也更仰賴補貼 ， 此將增加財政成本並減少外匯存底 ，
世銀預估中東石油出口要到 2027 年中才會恢復至戰前水準，能源成本上升已對
製造業造成壓力 ， 運輸價格大漲也已打擊消費需求 ， 持續性的補貼可能難以為繼 。

(2)印度:印度央行 10/7 貨幣政策會議宣布升息 1 碼至 5.5%，為近四年來首度升
息，貨幣政策立場從「中性」調整為「有節制的緊縮」。央行總裁指出，目前已
有跡象顯示，通膨預期升高，且價格壓力正逐漸擴散至更廣泛的領域。經濟展望
方面，印度內需、投資及基礎建設支出持續支撐景氣，央行將 2026/27 財年經濟
成長率預測由 6.7%上調至 7.1%，另受中東局勢緊張、國際油價上漲、食品價格
攀升及氣候因素影響，將 2026/27 財年 CPI 通膨率預測上調至 5.2%，高於 4%的
中期目標。另外，世界銀行 10 月最新《南亞經濟展望》報告，上修印度 2026/27
財年經濟成長率 0.4 個百分點至 7.1%，反映內需消費、製造業、服務業及出口表
現優於預期，然而，中東衝突導致能源價格上漲、聖嬰現象衝擊農業生產、全球
金融市場波動及資本流動風險，仍可能影響經濟前景。整體而言，印度經濟成長
動能仍穩健，但外部風險及輸入性通膨壓力增加，未來貨幣政策將以維持物價穩
定為優先，並有進一步升息的可能。 
 (3)越南: 2026 年第三季經濟成長年增率達 9.95%，高於上季修正後的 8.81%，並
創 4 年新高，累計前三季經濟成長率達 9.01%。成長動能主要來自製造業與營建
業擴張、外人直接投資持續流入、以及外貿大幅成長。各產業均穩健成長，工業
及建築業成長 12.50%(占 GDP 總值 51.57%)；服務業成長 9.54%(占 43.29%)；農
業成長 4.21%(占 5.14%)。以支出面觀之，民間消費成長 8.96%，固定投資成長
21.39%，貨物和服務出、進口分別成長23.27%、28.75%。展望未來，越南受惠於
全球企業分散供應鏈、AI 產業需求及政府推動基礎建設與數位轉型 ， 經濟前景仍
偏正向。

9

六、台灣—出口動能強勁，物價壓力與貿易風險並存 
9 月消費者物價指數(CPI)年增率升至 2.73%，創近兩年半新高，已連續 5 個
月高於 2%警戒線，主要係受豪雨影響，蔬果供應減少帶動價格上漲，加上汽機
車減稅效應淡化、中秋節落點不同及國際油價維持高檔，共同推升物價。除蔬果
外，外食費、房租、電費及交通相關費用皆持續上漲，顯示物價壓力仍在，但主
計總處認為整體漲勢仍屬溫和，預估 10 月 CPI 將略為回落至約 2.5%。 
9 月出口額達 872.2 億美元，年增 60.9%，其中資通產品出口倍增、電子零組
件年增 45.3%。財政部指出，在AI 相關投資持續、部分電子產品供應偏緊、新品
上市與旺季拉貨等因素共同挹注下，出口表現明顯升溫；隨著 AI 應用擴大及年
底採購需求增加，全年出口額有望突破 9,000 億美元。 
美國國際貿易委員會 （USITC）於10 月 9 日依據 《1930 年關稅法》 第337 條
款，對特定垂直供電系統及相關產品啟動專利侵權調查，其中台達電、廣達及鴻
海旗下相關企業等遭列名。若 337 條款調查最終認定侵權，相關產品可能遭禁止
輸入美國，後續應關注調查結果及對台灣電源供應與伺服器供應鏈的影響。

## Document 074: 2026年四季度宏观经济展望：分化延续

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年四季度宏观经济展望：分化延续.pdf
Report source: 中证鹏元
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 34
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

李雪伟
lixw@cspengyuan.com
主要内容：
核心观点：三季度逆周期政策加码，经济顶压前行，产需结构虽有分化，新动
能持续壮大。展望四季度，外部形势复杂严峻，地缘博弈持续演变，主要经济
体货币政策同步收紧，全球流动性边际收敛。国内经济加快转型升级，结构 K
型分化加剧，内需仍然不足。2026 年实现全年区间增长目标的难度不大，宏观
政策仍需要“发力提效”，加快落实一揽子存量政策和增量政策，增强经济发
展动能，保障经济平稳运行。财政政策要持续提高专项债、新型政策性金融工
具、地方债结存限额等资金的使用进度，聚焦“六张网”建设，助力稳投资，
统筹全年收官与跨年项目储备。货币政策适度宽松，降息降准可期，结构性工
具精准发力，择机推动政策利率换锚，强化预期引导，稳定人民币汇率。房地
产延续“控增量、去库存、优供给”主线，加快房地产业新模式落地。
一、2026 年前三季度宏观经济回顾：总体平稳，结构向优，内外需分化
前三季度宏观经济整体平稳，实际 GDP 同比增长 4.6%，位于 4.5%-5.0%的目

标区间。供给端偏强，需求端结构分化，消费偏弱，制造业投资和基建投资在
政策发力下降幅收窄，地产投资低位徘徊，出口端保持高景气。输入性通胀影
响生产端价格水平持续改善，居民端温和修复。货币政策延续适度宽松，结构
性工具精准发力。财政政策保持积极，用好存量同时，适时推出增量政策，注
重投资于人，激发私人部门内生动力。深化拓展“人工智能+”，推动科技自立
自强、产业链自主可控。
二、2026 年四季度宏观经济展望：边际修复，新旧动能转换延续，实现“十五
五”良好开局
（一）外部环境：美日欧货币政策从分化走向同步收紧，中期选举将重塑美国
两党格局。美国经济 K 型分化格局延续，美联储 9 月重启加息，将维持货币限
制性政策。欧元区经济弱复苏，短期抗通胀优先于稳增长。日本经济面临高利
率压制和债务约束，央行将继续渐进加息。地缘政治博弈持续，中东冲突进入
拉扯阶段，美国中期选举将重塑两党格局。
（二）内部环境：经济转型再平衡，供强需弱延续，企业盈利分化。当前宏观
分化延续—2026年四季度宏观经济展望

宏观研究
2026年 10 月 21 日

### Page 2

经济新旧动能加速转换，K 型分化延续。总需求边际增量高度依赖公共部门外
生拉动，私人部门持续处于防御性修复期，微观主体资本开支意愿钝化。供需
错配延续，高技术产业链的局部繁荣对内循环的溢出拉动作用有限，内需偏弱
仍是核心制约。受地缘与能源价格带来的输入性通胀影响，PPI 中枢再次被动
抬升，但在终端需求不足制约下价格向下游传导受阻，中下游实体企业盈利空
间遭到实质性挤压。
（三）宏观经济边际修复，预计增长目标顺利实现，实现“十五五”良好开局。
预计四季度经济整体延续回升，全年 GDP实际增速在 4.7%左右，落在目标区间
中枢偏下位置。四季度 PPI可能高位徘徊但总体呈下降趋势。生产端在科技制
造景气引领下边际修复；出口有望维持高增速，内需环比弱修复，房地产市场
维持低位震荡格局，基建投资和制造业投资有望降幅收窄回稳。海外需关注美
国中期选举扰动与中欧贸易摩擦；国内重点关注一揽子增量政策下降准降息等
宽松工具的出台节奏，以及 10 月底五中全会，12 月政治局会议与中央经济工
作会议对明年经济工作与增长目标的定调。

（四）宏观政策：加快资金使用，择机推动政策利率换锚，纵深推进“人工智
能+”。宏观政策延续稳增长、培育新质生产力、促消费等方面的政策导向，四
季度大幅加码的必要性不强，但仍需要“发力提效”。财政政策加快资金使用
进度，聚焦“六张网”建设，积极稳投资，统筹全年收官与跨年项目储备。货
币政策延续适度宽松，择机推动政策利率换锚。纵深推进“人工智能+”行动，
加快现代化产业体系建设。

### Page 3

核心观点：三季度逆周期政策加码，经济顶压前行，产需结构虽有分化，新动能持续壮大。展望四季度，
外部形势复杂严峻，地缘博弈持续演变，主要经济体货币政策同步收紧，全球流动性边际收敛。国内经济加
快转型升级，结构 K 型分化加剧，内需仍然不足。2026年实现全年区间增长目标的难度不大，宏观政策仍
需要“发力提效”，加快落实一揽子存量政策和增量政策，增强经济发展动能，保障经济平稳运行。财政政
策要持续提高专项债、新型政策性金融工具、地方债结存限额等资金的使用进度，聚焦“六张网”建设，助
力稳投资，统筹全年收官与跨年项目储备。货币政策适度宽松，降息降准可期，结构性工具精准发力，择机
推动政策利率换锚，强化预期引导，稳定人民币汇率。房地产延续“控增量、去库存、优供给”主线，加快
房地产业新模式落地。
一、2026 年前三季度宏观经济回顾：总体平稳，结构向优，内外需分化
（一）前三季度实际 GDP 同比增长 4.6%，位于 4.5%-5.0%的目标区间
前三季度经济增速整体保持平稳。初步核算，前三季度我国 GDP 总量为 1,071,071 亿元，按不变价格

计算的实际 GDP 同比增长 4.6%。分季度看，一季度实际 GDP 同比增长 5.0%，二季度同比增长 4.3%，三
季度同比增长 4.5%。从环比看，经季节因素调整后，三季度 GDP 环比增长 1.2%，较二季度回升 0.3个百分
点。分产业看，前三季度第一产业增加值 58,994亿元，同比增长 3.7%；第二产业增加值 383,822亿元，同
比增长 3.8%；第三产业增加值 628,255 亿元，同比增长 5.2%，三大产业对经济增长的贡献率分别为 4.6%、
29.1%和 66.3%，第三产业贡献率较上年同期提高 5.6个百分点，三次产业增加值占 GDP的比重分别为 5.5%、
35.8%和 58.7%。三大需求方面，前三季度消费支出对经济增长贡献率为 46%，拉动 GDP 增长 2.1 个百分
点，较去年同期有所回落；资本形成总额对经济增长贡献率为 28%，拉动 GDP 增长 1.3 个百分点；货物和
服务净出口对经济增长贡献率为 26%，拉动 GDP 增长 1.2个百分点。前三季度，全国居民人均可支配收入

34,230元，比上年同期名义增长 5.3%，实际增长 4.4%。

（二）结构性就业压力较大，PPI 与 CPI 剪刀差走阔
前三季度，全国城镇调查失业率平均值为 5.2%，和上年同期持平；9 月份，全国城镇调查失业率为 5.2%，
比上个月下降 0.1个百分点。31 个大城市城镇调查失业率为 5.2%，和上年同期持平。分年龄段来看，9 月
末（不含在校生）16-24 岁、25-29 岁、30-59 岁劳动力调查失业率分别为 18.0%、7.4%、3.9%，较去年同期
整体抬升，青年人面临的就业压力仍较大，重点群体的就业支持政策仍需持续发力。2026 前三季度全国企
业就业人员周平均工作时间为 48.3小时，较 2025年前三季度同比下降 0.3 小时。

前三季度，居民消费价格 CPI 同比上升 0.9%，其中 9 月全国居民消费价格同比上涨 0.9%，环比上涨
0.2%。前三季度，扣除食品和能源价格后的核心 CPI 同比上涨 1.1%。分类别看，食品烟酒及在外餐饮价格
同比下降 0.4%，衣着价格上涨 1.5%，居住价格下降 0.2%，生活用品及服务价格上涨 1.4%，交通通信价格
上涨 2.0%，教育文化娱乐价格上涨 1.2%，医疗保健价格上涨 2.3%，其他用品及服务价格上涨 9.8%。在食
品烟酒及在外餐饮价格中，猪肉价格下降 12.9%，粮食价格下降 0.4%，鲜果价格上涨 0.7%，鲜菜价格上涨
1.4%； 9 月末猪肉平均批发价为 16.42元/公斤，较上半年低点 14.28元/公斤有所抬升。从三季度来看，地
缘政治导致国际原油价格上涨冲击全球物价水平，从年初的 60美元/桶一度飙升至 3 月末的 118.35 美元/桶，
6月均价为 84.43美元/桶，9 月均价为 102.02美元/桶，环比上涨 17.59美元/桶。

前三季度，PPI 比上年同期上涨 2.3%，涨幅比上半年扩大 0.8个百分点。分月看，1、2 月份 PPI 同比分
别下降 1.4%和 0.9%；3月份，中东地缘政治冲突推高国际原油价格，PPI 同比转为上涨 0.5%；输入性因素
传导叠加国内外需求增加，4 月份 PPI 同比涨幅进一步扩大至 2.8%；5-8 月份 PPI 同比涨幅在 3.5%-4.1%之
间；9 月份，受国际能源价格快速上涨等因素影响，PPI 同比上涨 5.0%。前三季度，人工智能等领域加速发
展，算力网、新一代通信网等加快建设，带动相关元器件及装备制造等行业需求增加、价格上涨。

（三）信贷降速提质新常态，政府债发力托底社融增量
前三季度社会融资规模增量累计为 27.46 万亿元，比上年同期少增 2.63 万亿元，低于近三年同期均值，
整体融资需求仍显不足。具体分项来看，政府债券净融资仍是支撑社融增长的重要因素，企业债券融资表现
较为强劲，而人民币贷款增长相对偏弱。前三季度政府债券净融资 10.30 万亿元，同比少增 1.16 万亿元；
企业债券净融资 2.75 万亿元，同比多增 1.18 万亿元；社融口径人民币贷款增加 11.50 万亿元，同比少增
3.04 万亿元。9 月末社会融资规模存量为 468.17 万亿元，同比增长 7.1%，较上月下降 0.1 个百分点；M2
同比增长 7.2%，较上月下降 0.3 个百分点；M1 同比增长 3.2%，较上月下降 0.9个百分点，M2 与 M1 剪刀
差较上月走阔 0.6个百分点至 4.0 个百分点，社融与 M2 增速差收窄至-0.1 个百分点，反映货币活化程度有

所减弱，实体经济有效融资需求仍有待提振。
前三季度信贷增加 11.52 万亿元，比上年同期少增 3.23 万亿元，信贷投放整体偏弱。分部门看，居民
端信贷需求持续低迷，前三季度居民贷款累计减少 1.03 万亿元，同比少增 2.13 万亿元，其中短期贷款减少
1.14 万亿元，同比少增 0.91 万亿元，反映居民短期消费及经营融资需求偏弱；中长期贷款增加 1,062亿元，
同比少增 1.22 万亿元，居民中长期融资需求仍显不足。企业端贷款增加 12.55 万亿元，同比少增 0.89 万亿
元，其中短期贷款增加 4.64 万亿元，同比多增 0.11 万亿元；中长期贷款增加 6.53 万亿元，同比少增 1.76
万亿元，企业中长期融资需求偏弱；票据融资增加 1.24 万亿元，同比多增 0.76 万亿元，反映信贷结构仍有
待改善。非银行业金融机构贷款减少 5,865亿元。总体来看，前三季度信贷投放呈现居民端持续承压、企业

（四）供给端：工业生产季节性波动，现代服务业保持高景气扩张
供给端保持较快增长，前三季度全国规模以上工业增加值同比增长 5.3%，增速较上半年有所减缓，其
中 7-9 月同比分别增长 4.5%、5.2%和 5.5%，工业生产增速逐月回升。装备制造业和高技术制造业增势强劲，
增加值同比分别增长 10.2%和 14.3%，分别高于全部规模以上工业增加值增速 4.9 和 9.0 个百分点，新动能
对工业增长的支撑作用持续增强。分三大门类看，制造业增加值同比增长 5.7%，高于全部规模以上工业增
加值增速 0.4个百分点；采矿业和电力、热力、燃气及水生产和供应业分别增长 2.0%和 5.4%。分经济类型

看，国有控股企业增加值同比增长 3.8%，股份制企业增长 5.8%，外商及港澳台投资企业增长 3.2%，私营企
业增长 4.3%。分产品看，3D 打印设备、锂离子电池、工业机器人产品产量同比分别增长 62.5%、39.5%和

### Page 12

29.1%。制造业 PMI 前三季度围绕荣枯线波动，7-9 月分别为 49.2%、49.8%和 50.1%，9 月较上月回升 0.3
个百分点，重返扩张区间；其中生产指数为 51.7%，较上月上升 1.3个百分点，新订单指数为 50.5%，较上
月下降 0.1个百分点，生产端扩张有所加快，需求端修复仍有待巩固。1-8 月份，全国规模以上工业企业实
现利润总额 52,720 亿元，同比增长 15.7%，工业企业盈利修复动能放缓。
前三季度，服务业增加值同比增长 5.2%。其中，租赁和商务服务业，信息传输、软件和信息技术服务
业，金融业，住宿和餐饮业增加值分别增长 12.3%、10.4%、5.8%、5.5%。9 月份，全国服务业生产指数同
比增长 4.8%，比上月加快 0.7 个百分点。其中，信息传输、软件和信息技术服务业，租赁和商务服务业，交
通运输、仓储和邮政业，住宿和餐饮业生产指数分别增长 9.8%、9.5%、5.4%、5.3%。9月份，服务业商务
活动指数为 50.2%，比上月上升 0.9个百分点；服务业业务活动预期指数为 55.5%，与上月持平。其中，电

信广播电视及卫星传输服务、货币金融服务、保险等行业商务活动指数位于 55.0%以上较高景气区间。1-8
月份，规模以上服务业企业营业收入同比增长 6.0%。

（五）需求端：消费修复动能仍显不足，出口保持高景气，投资降幅收窄
从消费来看，前三季度社会消费品零售总额 370,001亿元，同比增长 1.1%，增速较上半年回落 0.2个百
分点，消费增长整体偏弱。9 月社会消费品零售总额为 42,433 亿元，同比增长 1.1%，较上月加快 0.7 个百
分点，消费增速有所回升。具体来看，9 月商品零售同比增长 1.0%，较 8月加快 0.7个百分点；餐饮收入同
比增长 2.1%，较 8 月加快 1.0 个百分点，商品和餐饮消费均有所改善。消费市场整体低位运行，从结构上
看，汽车消费相对偏弱，三季度扣除汽车类商品后的消费品零售额同比增长 3.0%，较二季度加快 1.0 个百
分点；基本生活类商品保持一定韧性，前三季度限额以上单位粮油食品类、服装鞋帽针纺织品类和日用品类
商品零售额同比分别增长 6.8%、4.6%和 3.8%。消费品以旧换新政策持续推进，智能绿色消费表现较好，通

讯器材类和文化办公用品类商品零售额同比分别增长 18.1%和 6.2%。网络消费和乡村消费保持较快增长，
前三季度网上商品零售额 95,060 亿元，同比增长 3.8%，其中吃类、穿类、用类商品零售额分别增长 15.8%、
3.4%和 0.8%；按经营单位所在地看，前三季度城镇消费品零售额 319,820亿元，同比增长 0.9%，乡村消费
品零售额 50,181 亿元，同比增长 2.3%，乡村消费增速快于城镇 1.4个百分点。服务消费仍保持较快增长，
前三季度服务零售额同比增长 5.0%，高于同期商品零售额增速 4.0 个百分点。
从外贸来看，前三季度我国进出口总值 5.80 万亿美元，同比增长 23.8%。其中，出口端保持较高景气
度，出口 3.37万亿美元，同比增长 21.3%，较上半年增长 3.8个百分点，其中 7-9 月出口同比分别增长 23.9%、
24.9%和 36.3%，三季度出口增速逐月回升；进口 2.43 万亿美元，同比增长 27.5%，进口增速快于出口 6.2
个百分点，进出口保持较快增长。从贸易市场结构看，前三季度我国对共建“一带一路”国家进出口金额同

比增长 17.1%，对欧盟、韩国进出口金额分别同比增长 12.9%和 59.2%，对美国进出口金额 4,625.42 亿美元，

### Page 14

同比增长 8.6%，多元化市场布局持续深化。从经营主体看，前三季度我国国有企业、外商投资企业和私营
企业进出口金额分别同比增长 25.0%、26.3%和 24.0%，各类市场主体进出口均保持较快增长。从出口产品
结构看，前三季度机电产品出口同比增长 23.7%，电动汽车、锂电池等绿色产品出口分别增长 72.3%和 36.4%，
电子元件、电线电缆出口分别增长 70.0%和 25.3%，机电、绿色及新兴技术相关产品出口表现亮眼，出口结
构持续优化。
从投资来看，前三季度全国固定资产投资（不含农户）334,115亿元，同比下降 7.2%，较上半年降幅扩
大 1.5个百分点，投资整体持续承压。分领域看，前三季度基础设施投资同比下降 6.1%，较 2025 年同期回
落 7.6 个百分点；制造业投资同比下降 2.1%，较 2025 年同期回落 4.8个百分点；房地产开发投资同比下降
20.6%，较 2025年同期降幅扩大 5.9个百分点，三大领域投资增速较上半年均有所回落。前三季度全国新建
商品房销售面积 56,483 万平方米，同比下降 13.2%；新建商品房销售额 53,799 亿元，同比下降 13.9%，房

地产市场仍处于调整阶段。同时，投资结构仍存在一定亮点，知识产权产品投资同比增长 9.3%，拉动全部
投资增长 1.2个百分点；设备工器具购置投资同比增长 9.7%，拉动全部投资增长 1.6个百分点。高技术产业
投资同比增长 4.9%，其中高技术制造业和高技术服务业投资分别增长 4.0%和 6.6%，电子电路制造业、锂
离子电池制造业及集成电路制造业投资分别增长 58.7%、20.9%和 14.2%。重点基础设施领域投资表现较好，
互联网和相关服务业、航空运输业、水上运输业及电力供应业投资分别增长 46.7%、13.8%、13.5%和 11.2%。

二、2026 年四季度宏观经济展望：边际修复，新旧动能转换延续，实现“十五五”良好开局
（一）外部形势：美日欧货币政策从分化走向同步收紧，中期选举将重塑美国两党格局
美国经济 K型分化格局延续，美联储将维持货币限制性政策。二季度 GDP环比折年增速小幅下行 0.3pct

### Page 16

至 2.2%，主要受到个人消费支出和人工智能相关的私人投资拉动，其中私人投资对 GDP 增速的贡献较一季
度小幅回落，但仍处较高位置；政府支出下滑形成部分抵消，进口上升拖累净出口贡献。三季度非农就业回
落，平均时薪同环比增长小幅放缓，劳动力市场需求边际降温，但失业率处于低位，就业市场相对平衡；部
分行业因 AI 的替代效应出现结构性失业，不过尚未形成明显冲击。通胀方面，能源价格继续主导价格波动，
7-9 月 CPI 同比由 3.4%反弹至 3.7%，9 月核心 CPI 同比上行至 2.6%，高于市场预期，核心通胀粘性仍在。
展望后续，美国经济在财富效应支撑的居民消费、AI 资本开支扩张等带动下有望保持较强韧性，高油价对
经济的冲击相对有限，但持续的高利率也将抑制企业和居民信贷需求，房地产、传统制造业等利率敏感型行
业与 AI 产业的 K 型分化格局将延续。通胀方面，能源价格走势存在不确定性，其中原油价格有低库存和战
略储备回补需求的支撑，中东冲突存在反复，通胀中枢预计整体抬升，回落周期将拉长。货币政策上，美联

储 9 月启动加息，收回前期部分宽松。四季度通胀处于相对高位，较 2%的政策目标仍有距离，美联储将维
持货币限制性政策，基准情形下 12月或有一次加息，未来具体的加息路径和幅度仍主要取决于美伊冲突进
展和通胀走势。财政方面，虽然联邦赤字维持高位，但是为刺激经济、应对中期选举，美国财政扩张基调难
以系统性逆转。此外，《大而美法案》等前期政策形成的减税红利在 2026年达到峰值，若无新一轮财政刺
激，财政脉冲将由正转负，财政货币双重收紧对经济的拖累效应可能将逐步显现。
欧元区经济弱复苏，短期抗通胀优先于稳增长。欧元区经济弱复苏延续，面临“低增长、高通胀”的滞
胀压力。今年二季度实际 GDP 同比增长 1.2%，较一季度上升 0.6pct，主要拉动来自货物和服务出口，内需
消费增速平稳。随着不确定性消退、市场信心改善，三季度欧元区经济活动较二季度出现修复，9 月经济景
气指数回升至 97.9，ZEW 经济景气指数大幅上升至 25.8，中东冲突带来的负面冲击低于预期；欧洲消费者
信心边际增强，服务业 PMI 三季度重回扩张区间，欧元区消费者信心指数从 6 月的-17.7回升至 9 月的 16.5；

制造业保持高景气，欧元区制造业 PMI 连续 8 个月处于扩张区间，而且行业景气度较二季度上行。欧元区
各国经济分化，德国受益于国防支出、AI 建设而增长较快，法国受到极端天气的拖累。展望后续，作为能
源进口依赖型地区，欧洲仍面临俄乌战争、美伊冲突等地缘风险持续带来的外部不确定性，同时全球需求回
落、欧洲与中美贸易摩擦风险将压制出口动能；能源价格将沿产业链传导，对制造业产业链的长期结构性损
伤难以在短期内修复。财政方面，德国财政扩张力度最大，对经济拉动效应显著；法国、意大利等部分高债
务国财政压力仍存，赤字率维持高位，面临一定的债务可持续风险。欧元区经济依赖内生消费需求，难以实
现强劲反弹；相对于 2025 年 1.5%的增速，欧央行 9 月预测 2026年 GDP 增速为 0.9%，整体增速延续放缓
趋势。货币政策上，欧央行 6 月开启近三年来的首次加息，9 月再次加息，旨在确保通胀在中期内稳定在 2%
的目标水平，当前货币政策目标中抗通胀优先于稳增长。四季度油价或维持高位震荡，而且存在能源价格反

弹引发通胀二次传导的风险，欧央行通胀压力并未缓解，预计将保持货币政策紧缩状态，市场定价 12 月仍
有加息 25 个基点的可能。

### Page 17

日本经济面临高利率压制和债务约束，央行将继续渐进加息。日本经济呈现微弱复苏态势，二季度 GDP
环比折年率为 1.4%，连续三个季度正增长，但低于预期，内需疲软与外部需求的结构性分化成为核心特征，
人工智能的旺盛需求未能完全对冲内需的下滑。三季度日本制造业景气度保持高位，制造业 PMI 均处在扩
张区间，地缘扰动不改制造业景气的上升趋势。通胀方面，油价、海外大宗商品、日元贬值等推升进口成本，
劳动力紧平衡支撑薪资上行，日本 CPI 和核心 CPI 三季度上行；受益于政府补贴对能源和食品涨价的缓冲
效果，1-8 月均未超过 2%的通胀目标。四季度输入型通胀压力持续，政府补贴退坡导致电费、燃气费等价
格出现跳涨，没有新增政策的情况下通胀面临上行压力。货币政策方面，日本央行 9 月如期加息 25BP，将
政策目标利率提至近 31 年新高。行长植田和男最新表示核心通胀已经“十分接近”央行设定的 2%通胀目
标，存在进一步加息的空间。日本央行可能倾向避免连续加息，但不排除 12月再度加息的可能性。首相高

市早苗倾向于偏鸽派的货币政策以支持经济增长，支持扩张性财政刺激，如额外支出、结构性减税和补贴；
其上任以来推出多项支出方案，包括为期两年的食品消费税减免、面向战略产业的长期投资计划等。不过日
本货币政策受到央行独立性和通胀数据的制约，财政政策受限于高债务和刚性支出，实际扩张规模或相对
有限。整体来看，日本经济面临高利率压制和债务约束，日元贬值风险仍存，增长动力主要来自内需的温和
复苏和全球 AI 产业周期的拉动，GDP 预计保持低速增长；日本央行将继续推进渐进加息，未来半年内或有
一次加息。
地缘政治博弈持续，中东冲突进入拉扯阶段，美国中期选举将重塑两党格局。全球地缘局势呈现局部
冲突频发、重大战争可控化的特点，大国博弈进入相对稳定阶段。美伊双方进入边打边谈的拉扯阶段，以色
列间歇性干扰和谈进程；虽然美伊双方有意管控分歧，但双方分歧巨大，后续局部冲突难以避免，不确定性
仍存，中期选举后存在冲突升级的风险。在伊朗局势稳定后，美国或将目光转移至古巴等地区。俄乌冲突陷
入焦灼状态，期间有和谈信号释放，但和平进程较难推进。日本保守化倾向持续加深，高市早苗政府推动修

宪和军事松绑，持续在核问题边界试探，而且对华战略进一步向“全面防范优先”倾斜，东亚地区军备竞赛
风险增加。中美博弈进入“斗而不破”的阶段性平衡，难改长期竞争和博弈的本质；5 月元首峰会后两国进
入新的平台期，下半年延续高频沟通态势，给中美关系注入更多稳定剂。此外，11 月美国中期选举可能迎
来反转，民主党拿下两院的概率上升至六成。特朗普面临支持率低迷、民众生活成本高企等不利局面，美伊
冲突的焦灼局面消耗其政治根基。按照趋势，民主党在中期选举拿回众议院的概率较高，参议院归属主要看
少数摇摆州的选择。不过即使是民主党横扫两院，由于未掌握绝大多数，未来两年其对美国的影响主要在程
序层面而非立法，实质影响集中在拨款权，即民主党主导众议院时，后续军费与扩大战事的授权会明显受
限。两党博弈加剧，政府停摆、债务上限危机发生的风险上升。

（二）内部环境：经济转型再平衡，供强需弱延续
新旧动能加速转换，结构性分化延续。三季度，宏观经济结构调整阵痛延续，传统板块出清继续拖累总
需求。前三季度房地产开发投资完成额累计同比降幅扩大至 20.60%，并沿产业链向上压制原材料与中上游
工业需求。基础原材料行业 PMI 在三季度持续处于收缩区间，黑色金属冶炼及压延加工业、非金属矿物制
品业利润大幅下滑。科技制造主线则保持较强韧性，高技术制造业 PMI 持续运行在高景气区间。新旧动能
转换的非对称性在内外需中进一步凸显。外需受全球 AI 与算力周期驱动，集成电路出口同比增长 108.10%，

### Page 23

数据处理设备出口增长 54.60%，直接拉动电子设备制造业前八个月利润同比大增 109.90%。而传统板块及
劳动密集型产业链则持续承压，地产后周期陶瓷产品出口下降 27.20%，鞋靴出口亦录得 2.50%的负增长。
由外需和科技周期驱动的增量动能短期内难以完全弥补传统内需走弱的缺口，经济再平衡诉求更为凸显。
供需错配延续，内需仍然是核心制约。生产端在三季度整体“先抑后稳”，季初受极端天气及淡季扰动
阶段性承压，9月随着扰动消退及政策托底，工业增加值当月同比回升至 5.50%，制造业 PMI 生产指数反弹
至扩张区间。尽管季末生产企稳，但高技术产业链的繁荣对传统制造业及国内大循环的溢出拉动作用并不
显著。消费端商品弱、服务强特征延续，前三季度商品零售累计同比仅录得 1%，而服务零售同比增长 5%。
叠加地产周期拖累，9月建筑及装潢材料类零售额同比下跌 9.70%，整体内生消费动能尚未得到实质性扭转。
投资端当前面临的主要问题在于优质项目储备不足及实物工作量转化时滞。9月初“六张网”基建规划明确、

政府债券发行提速，政策性金融工具实质性投放，但核心工作量主要落在四季度，未能完全拉动三季度表
现，前三季度全国固定资产投资完成额累计同比仍下降 7.20%，仅 9 月制造业投资降幅收窄至 0.90%表现出
局部企稳迹象。
内需结构“公强私弱”，边际增量高度依赖公共部门外生拉动。从私人部门来看，居民端资产负债表持
续防御性修复。受收入预期下修约束，前三季度全国人均可支配收入实际累计同比录得 4.4%，虽有所抬升
但依然偏低，导致居民加杠杆意愿显著放缓，前三季度居民中长期贷款仅新增 1,062 亿元，同比大幅少增
12,238亿元。企业端受制于终端需求不足与资本回报率预期偏弱，资本开支意愿钝化，前三季度民间固定资
产投资累计同比大幅下探至-10.4%的历史低位，微观主体经营重心普遍转向降本增效与债务去化。与私人部
门信用收缩相对应，政府部门加杠杆权力托底，前三季度政府债券融资在社融增量中的占比达 37.5%。三季
度末，财政金融协同促内需持续加码，通过盘活并动用地方债务结存限额保障支出强度，新型政策性金融工

具开始实质性投放。同时，财政支出逻辑边际优化，向投资于人与直达终端倾斜，通过贷款贴息、定向补贴
等方式直接降低微观主体融资成本与刚性支出负担。
输入性通胀再次推高 PPI 读数，企业上下游盈利分化加剧。叠加全球地缘博弈与供给端约束，国际能
源价格高位运行成为扰动国内物价的核心外生变量。9 月以来，布伦特原油期货结算价再次突破 100 美元/
桶关口，直接抬升国内能源品价格，PPI 当月同比在 9 月冲高至 5.00%，带动前三季度 PPI 累计同比达 2.3%。
与此同时，受制于内生有效需求不足，核心 CPI 表现依然疲弱，9月当月同比仅录得 1.00%。PPI 与核心 CPI
剪刀差持续走阔，映射出上游成本抬升无法顺畅向下游传递的价格传导阻滞。此外，上游资源品行业直接受
益于输入性涨价逻辑，盈利表现强劲；反观中下游实体企业，则面临原材料成本高企与终端需求疲弱的双头
挤压，盈利遭到实质性侵蚀，8月汽车制造、家具制造等下游行业利润同比分别深跌 16.00%与 51.80%。这
种成本冲击导致中下游企业资本回报率持续承压，进一步压制了实体经济的扩表动能。

（三）宏观经济边际修复，预计增长目标顺利实现，实现“十五五”良好开局
四季度经济整体预计延续回升，全年经济增速在 4.7%左右，实现“十五五”良好开局。四季度全球经
济和贸易形势仍趋复杂严峻，地缘政治冲突持续，能源价格持续高位加剧通胀和供应链风险，主要经济体进

### Page 27

入加息周期。国内经济延续 K 型分化，新旧动能持续转换，外需和高端制造业托举大盘，内需虽有改善但
变化不明显。四季度国内宏观政策仍需积极发力，进一步扩内需和稳经济，预计全年 GDP 实际增速在 4.7%
左右，基本落在 4.5%-5.0%目标区间的中间偏下位置。价格水平方面，受结构性油价和输入型通胀影响，9
月 PPI 再次冲涨，进一步压缩中下游利润；四季度 PPI 可能高位徘徊，但总体价格水平将呈现高点下降趋
势，后续需重点关注中东局势和油价变化。四季度国内重点关注一揽子增量政策下，降准降息等总量型宽松
工具的出台节奏，聚焦 10 月底五中全会，以及 12 月政治局会议和中央经济工作会议对明年经济工作与增
长目标的定调。
生产端边际修复，新质生产力延续高景气引领。三季度极端天气与制造业传统淡季的扰动后，叠加增量
政策的托底效应，四季度生产端有望迎来边际修复。新旧动能转换稳步推进，科技制造主线动能强劲。在出
口产业链拉动与全球 AI 算力周期的共振下，以半导体及高端电子设备为主导的高技术制造业持续繁荣，对

工业供给大盘形成坚实支撑。但与此同时，工业生产内部的结构性分化依然显著。科技制造的高景气对传统
制造产业链的外溢带动作用相对有限，传统高载能行业与部分中下游加工制造仍面临内需偏弱与成本压制
的双重约束。四季度生产端的全面提质增效，仍有赖于增量政策向终端有效需求的加速传导。
出口有望继续维持高增速，内需环比弱修复。出口方面，受低基数效应和全球 AI 周期的持续拉动，四
季度出口有望维持 20%以上的高增速，继续充当宏观大盘的核心托举力量，但出口内部同样存在显著的结
构性分化。国内大循环方面，内需预计环比有所修复，但居民预期和微观体感依然不强，总体仍偏弱，尤其
是消费低迷和房地产投资下行的拖累仍在延续。后续促消费的重点需放在大力发展服务消费、拓展消费亮
点，并切实提高居民的收入预期上。投资端，固定资产投资有望降幅收窄并逐步回稳。9 月初启动的“六张
网”规划、政府债加速发行及政策性金融工具投放，其实物工作量将集中在四季度兑现，带动基建与制造业
投资边际企稳。房地产投资端，开发投资、新开工及销售等核心指标仍在低位徘徊，短期难言实质性反转，

但政策密集催化已带来预期拐点，预计后续修复过程大概率将延续城市间的分化特征，不同能级城市的复
苏节奏与力度将进一步拉开差距。9 月一线城市新房单月同比增速转正，10 月初京沪深等核心城市二手房
成交放量反弹，活跃度超过去两年同期。随着首套房财政贴息政策落地，四季度有望脉冲式撬动中小户型刚
需入市，对成交形成边际托底。预计四季度房地产市场整体维持弱复苏、强分化的低位震荡格局，但需警惕
二手房“以价换量”对价格体系的持续压制。

（四）宏观政策：聚焦扩内需和增量举措
宏观政策延续稳增长、培育新质生产力、促消费等方面的政策导向，投资端以“六张网”和重大项目形
成实物工作量，金融端以补充银行资本和政策性金融工具打开信用扩张空间，消费端以财政贴息和服务业
扩能提质双向发力，着力优化经济结构与提升政策效能。在“努力完成全年经济社会发展目标任务”的要求
下，四季度实际经济增速最低只需达到 4.3%左右，即可支撑全年增速达到 4.5%的下限，宏观政策大幅加码
的必要性不强，但仍需要“发力提效”，加快落实一揽子存量政策和增量政策，增强经济发展动能，同时及

### Page 29

时出台储备政策，确定经济平稳运行。
货币政策：先结构后总量，择机推动政策利率换锚。三季度央行延续适度宽松的政策立场，逆周期调节
加码，结构性工具精准发力；隔夜逆回购操作常态化，DR001 围绕政策利率中枢波动，短端利率稳定性显
著提升。央行未出台总量政策，而是通过 MLF 加量续作、隔夜逆回购扩容、PSL 降息扩围、两项再贷款增
额四条路径定向发力，同时在需求端配合财政部门推出居民购房贷款贴息。展望后续，经济处于新旧动能转
换的阵痛期，货币政策继续保持支持性立场，为内需回暖和产业转型创造适宜的货币金融环境。货币政策延
续“先结构后总量”，年内降准降息仍可期。结构性工具继续强化，加力支持扩内需、科技创新、中小微企
业等重点领域。隔夜利率锚定强化，择机推动政策利率换锚，深化货币价格型调控。设立应对非银机构大额
赎回的流动性支持工具，及时为机构提供流动性托底。强化预期引导，稳定人民币汇率，防范跨境资本波动
冲击国内流动性环境。
财政政策：加快存量政策落地，统筹全年收官与跨年项目储备。为实现“十五五”良好开局，财政政策

作为逆周期核心抓手，需要加快资金使用和政策协同，统筹全年收官与跨年项目储备。具体来看：（1）总
量政策继续落实“更加积极”的主基调，广义财政保持高强度；用好用足存量政策，根据需要及时储备增量
政策，如提前下达 2027年新增地方债限额，延续两项年底到期的消费贴息政策，或追加相关预算安排；（2）
加快资金的使用进度。加紧推动 109项重大工程、“六张网”项目、“两重”建设进度，持续提高专项债、
新型政策性金融工具、地方债结存限额等资金的使用进度，项目端和资金端齐发力，撬动更多社会资本，尽
早形成实物工作量。（3）支出结构继续优化，坚持投资于物和投资于人并重：一方面投向新型能源、算力
基础设施、产业链补短板工程；另一方面持续加码民生、公共服务和就业扶持，提升教育、医疗、社保、养
老等支出占比，夯实内循环基础。（4）落实防风险和基层保障，兜牢“三保”底线，多措并举推进化债攻
坚，持续压降地方隐性债务，严控新增。（5）加强财政贴息与结构性货币工具、专项债资本金与银行配套
融资的协同配合，保持财政政策、货币政策和产业政策取向一致性，促进政策同向发力。（6）统筹全年收

官与跨年项目储备：超前谋划一批符合政策导向、前期工作扎实的优质项目，为明年财政起好步赢得先机。
产业政策：纵深推进“人工智能+”行动，加快现代化产业体系建设。围绕科技自立自强和新质生产力
培育，政策以“人工智能+”为核心抓手，优化传统产业和新兴产业布局，以科技创新引领新质生产力发展，
以深化“反内卷”来规范市场秩序，构建智能化、绿色化、融合化的现代产业体系。具体看：一是纵深推进
“人工智能+”行动，加快人工智能商业化规模化应用和生态建设，全方位赋能千行百业；分层推进六大新
兴支柱产业规模化，强化产业链上下游协同、区域错位布局。二是前瞻布局未来产业，完善投入增长与风险
分担机制，加大基础研究投入，抢占技术制高点。三是加码传统产业智能化绿色化改造，落实设备更新和节
能降碳补贴，推动钢铁、化工等重点行业产能置换以及能效升级；稳步推进新型能源体系建设，把握能源安
全主动权。四是持续落地“反内卷”和统一大市场配套政策，建立重点行业产能监测预警，通过标准、能耗、

### Page 30

准入引导供需平衡；分层分类壮大现代服务业，培育“中国服务”品牌，促进生产性服务业和先进制造业融
合。
房地产政策：延续“控增量、去库存、优供给”主线，加快房地产业新模式落地。2026年前三季度，
全国房地产市场延续总量寻底、结构分化的格局，核心一二线城市在政策加持下释放改善性需求，核心地段
项目韧性更强，二手房成交持续优于新房；三四线城市库存压力高、去化缓慢；全国房企新开工、拿地意愿
整体偏弱，地产投资同比跌幅尚未收敛，居民资产负债表修复进程缓慢。政策主线延续“控增量、去库存、
优供给”，同时增量政策取得突破：“929”房产政策为中央财政首次为房贷贴息，标志着房地产调控政策
从间接调控转为直接补贴，和“828”现房销售政策分别从需求端和供给端发力，短期将提振刚需成交，中
长期将加速供给侧出清，推动小户型房价早日止跌企稳。为更好适应房地产市场新格局，加快构建房地产发
展新模式，需要从供需两端继续发力。一方面，持续盘活存量，加大存量商品房收购转化保障房和安置房力
度，用好配套专项债；供地结构持续向核心区、好房子项目倾斜，严控远郊低效新增供地；优化房企融资支

持，保交房白名单动态扩容，落实资金封闭监管，优先保障已售项目交付。另一方面，因城施策继续优化限
制性政策，支持核心城市适时调整核心区限购、限贷；增加公积金贷款、购房补贴、存量房贷利率、交易税
负等方面的优惠力度，支持住房“以旧换新”，激活购房刚需和改善性需求；引导更多城市加快公积金政策
优化调整，提高贷款额度，扩大提取和缴存覆盖范围；针对初婚初育家庭、多子女家庭、青年人才等特殊群
体，推出差异化购房补贴、公积金贴息等政策，支持其住房需求。此外，“929”贴息后的房贷利率和公积
金贷款利率差异不大，居民购房成本边际下降，调降公积金贷款利率的时间预计推迟，短期出台的概率可能
不高。

## Document 103: 261027_daiwa_TPCA

Source PDF: all report pdf/261027_daiwa_TPCA.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-27
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: We attended the 2026 Taiwan Printed Circuit Association (TPCA) Show that 
was held on 20-22 October. Below, we share our read-throughs from booth visits and key 
company takeaways. Overall, we remain positive on Taiwan glass fibre, CCL, PCB, and 
substrate vendors, supported by upbeat company commentary. On pricing, we expect 
low-end CCL and E-glass price increases to be larger and more sustained than previously 
anticipated. High-end CCL vendor commentary suggests plans for more meaningful price 
increases from 2H26 to maintain their products’ relative profitability amid sharp increases 
in low-end CCL prices. We also expect prices for T-glass, ABF, CCL, and substrates to 
continue rising. On the CCL technology roadmap, we favour Q-glass combined with 
hydrocarbon resin (HC) over PTFE-based alternatives as the longer-term direction. Lastly, 
we provide our view on the CCL market competition landscape change after seeing 
Nan Ya Plastics targeting the high-end market more aggressively. We also provide some of 
our key highlights from related companies within the Taiwan PCB supply chain. EMC (2383 
TT, TWD4,750, Buy [1]), ITEQ (6213 TT, TWD746, Buy [1]), Kinsus (3189 TT, TWD941, 
Buy [1]) and NYPCB (8046 TT, TWD1,255, Buy [1]) are our top ideas within our coverage.

We attended the 2026 Taiwan Printed Circuit Association (TPCA) Show last week. Below, 
we share our read-throughs from booth visits and key company takeaways.

Whether to adopt PTFE emerged as a key point of debate at the show.  
• During the TPCA show, Taiwan Union Technology (TUC; 6274 TT, TWD1,280, Buy [1]) 
unveiled its new M10 (Hydrocarbon base) and M11 (PTFE base) materials with glass-
fabric (Low-Dk2, Q-glass) and glass-fabric-free versions. Nan Ya Plastic (1303 TT, not 
rated) also unveiled its M11-grade material (ie, NPG-A99) at the show. Based on our 
understanding, NPG-A99 will continue to a hydrocarbon resin (HC) base, likely 
combined with Low-Dk2, to enhance CCL performance for high frequency and high-
speed signal transmission requirements. However, Elite Material (EMC; 2383 TT, 
TWD4,750, Buy [1]) remains reluctant to enter the PTFE-based CCL market, citing 
limited end application. Also, EMC plans to use advanced Q-glass for its M10 CCL. 
Lastly, we also heard from Panasonic’s booth representative that Panasonic (6752 JP, 
JPY3,687, Neutral [3]; covered by Ayaka Inomata) is pursuing a similar strategy for 
M9/M10 development, leveraging Q-glass instead of PTFE at this stage.

• While Nvidia (NVDA US, USD216, Outperform [2]; covered by Louis Miscioscia) 
appears keen to enhance product performance by adopting PTFE, given its very low 
dissipation factor (Df), PCB manufacturing yields remain a key concern. Accordingly, 
some leading vendors are broadening their material options (including PTFE-based 
CCL) to address potential customer requirements. EMC and Panasonic, however, 
remain firmly committed to a Q-glass-based technology roadmap. Although TUC 
highlighted progress on its PTFE-based M11 at a technology conference during the 
show, we believe it also intends to advance its existing HC resin/Low-Dk2 and HC 
resin/Q-glass roadmap for its next-generation CCL. In our view, its PTFE-based M11 
development is intended to preserve flexibility across potential technology pathways 
rather than replace its existing approach. Nan Ya Plastics’s strategy also appears 
reasonable: it can produce Low-Dk glass in-house but must source Q-glass and PTFE 
externally. This sourcing structure may explain its continued use of Low-Dk2 with HC-
resin instead of PTFE for its next-gen products, including M11.

 Taiwan PCB: Our key takeaways from 2026 TPCA show

### Page 2

Taiwan Data Centre Hardware 
27 October 2026 
2

• Conclusion: which technology roadmap will prevail? We believe it is too early to 
identify a clear winner, as the supply chain continues to improve manufacturing yields 
for PTFE-based products. Some vendors remain cautious despite PTFE’s long history in 
the industry, as its thermal shrinkage and relative softness still make adoption in high-
layer-count (HLC) PCBs challenging. Given the current stance of leading global vendors 
EMC, Doosan (000150 KS), and Panasonic, together with EMC’s execution track 
record, we currently favour Q-glass-based solutions without PTFE as the more likely 
direction. Nevertheless, broadening product offerings to address different customer 
requirements should not be viewed negatively, provided vendors have sufficient 
resources to support development across multiple technology pathways.

Low-end CCL and e-glass continue to surprise on the upside.  
• During the show, we also spoke with Kingboard Laminates (1888 HK, not rated). The 
company remained positive on the E-glass outlook, although its booth representative 
provided no numerical guidance for next year following a c.200% price hike in 2026. 
Kingboard attributed the pricing strength to supply constraints, driven largely by AI 
server demand. While we highlighted increased glass yarn supply from Chinese peers in 
2Q/3Q26, the company argued that the weaving machine capacity remains the 
bottleneck. It expects to add only c.500 weaving machines in 2026 and 1.2k in 2027 (vs. 
current installed base of 3.3k). E-glass fabric price increases therefore appear 
supported by limited weaving capacity and producers shifting capacity from lower-end 
glass to higher-end products, including Low-Dk, Low-Dk2, and T-glass. Kingboard also 
noted a similar trend in low-end copper foil, where shortages have recently intensified.

• The read-through from Nan Ya Plastics is similar. The company indicated that its current 
fibreglass allocation between internal consumption and external sales is 45:55, with the 
internal share set to rise gradually as it upgrades its product mix. Its CCL portfolio 
remains concentrated in FR-4 (M2 grade), but it plans to migrate toward M4/M6, 
supported by long-term contract wins with two major hyperscalers seeking to secure 
CCL supply. In 2027, M4/M6 products are expected to account for 60-70% of its mix (4m 
out of 6.5m in capacity), with M2 and below contributing 20%. The remainder would 
comprise high-end CCL, including M7/M8/M9, reflecting recent development progress. 
We therefore expect Nan Ya’s external thin-type E-glass supply to decline as its M4/M6 
mix increases next year.

Competition from Nan Ya Plastics.   
• The current CCL shortage creates favourable conditions for Nan Ya Plastics to expand 
into the high-end CCL supply chain. Whether the company has entered AMZN’s T3/T4 
supply chain is also a key point of market debate. Based on discussions with its booth 
representatives, however, we believe management will prioritise upgrading its M4/M6 
product portfolio before pursuing the highest-end CCL market more aggressively. On the 
positive side, favourable supply-demand dynamics have supported Nan Ya’s transition 
from a low-end supplier toward the mid-to-high-end segment, although high-end CCL 
should still contribute a relatively small share of revenue in 2027. For incumbent 
suppliers, competition is clearly intensifying, but we expect the near-term impact on 
high-end peers to remain limited.

• Our read: We expect the impact on EMC and TUC to remain relatively limited in 2027. 
Competitive pressure could become more meaningful over 2028-2030, although it is too 
early to assess its magnitude.

### Page 3

Taiwan Data Centre Hardware 
27 October 2026 
3

Other key takeaways: 
• Nitto Boseki (3110 JP, JPY2,942, Neutral [3]; covered by Noritsugu Hirakawa) identified 
Taiwan Glass (1802 TT, NR) and Grace Fabric Technology (603256 CH, CNY84.95, 
Outperform [2]; covered by Dennis Ip) as key competitors. Its plan to triple capacity by 
2028 remains largely on track. The company also intends to expand capacity further by 
2030, although the details have yet to be confirmed.

• Resonac (4004 JP, JPY12,915, Outperform [2]; covered by Hidemitsu Umebayashi) 
indicated that customers are requesting ABF substrate core thicknesses of 2,000-
3,000μm, versus its current maximum of 1,600μm, to accommodate larger package 
sizes. Potential technology pathways include adopting glass cores or ceramic 
substrates, or stacking additional core layers to enhance rigidity. Based on discussions 
with booth representatives, substrate makers may move toward multi-core structures, 
which could increase T-glass consumption. However, technical challenges in enabling 
signal transmission between core layers remain a potential barrier that would need to be 
addressed over the next one to two years.

• Mitsui Kinzoku (5706 JP, JPY2,054, Outperform [2]; covered by Shinichiro Ozaki) 
indicated that, based on its technology roadmap, M10-grade CCL combined with its 
HVLP5 copper foil should support 448Gbps SerDes transmission. In the near term, the 
company plans to shift capacity from lower-end to higher-end HVLP products, with a key 
piece of production equipment scheduled for installation in 2028/29. On pricing, its booth 
representative suggested that quarterly increases of more than 10% would be 
appropriate. Regarding copper foil shortages highlighted by other supply-chain 
participants, the representative indicated that mid-tier products, such as HVLP1 and 
HVLP2, could face the most severe supply constraints.

• Unimicron (3037 TT, TWD1,060, Buy [1]) appeared to highlight multi-core structures and 
the embedding of passive components within core layers as key technology trends in 
ABF substrates.

• Zhen Ding Technology (ZDT; 4958 TT, not rated) indicated that its substrate business 
has a plan to list on the Hong Kong Stock Exchange in 1H27 under the name Leading 
Interconnect Semiconductor Technology (LIST). At the show, the company focused 
primarily on thick HDI boards and highlighted two next-generation technology trends: 1) 
sequential lamination (M+N) to improve signal integrity and increase routing density 
within the same footprint; and 2) hybrid HDI/HLC structures, which add HDI layers to 
thick HLC boards (M+N), as discussed earlier.

## Document 112: 261027_ubs_faraday

Source PDF: all report pdf/261027_ubs_faraday.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-27
Pages: 13
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

ab 27 October 2026
Global Research
First Read
Faraday Technology
New opportunities in advanced nodes
Q326 beat on turnkey strength and non-op gains; Q426 outlook positive
Q326 EPS of NT$1.07 came in above UBS/Street expectations mainly on  non-op gains.
Q326 sales came in at NT$3.66 bn (+11% QoQ), ahead of its original guidance but in
line with its comment  during Taiwan Summit with turnkey sales up 20% QoQ given
better support from OSAT and foundries, despite NRE declining 31% QoQ. Q326 GM

decreased 2.7ppt QoQ to 42.5% on higher turnkey mix, while  OPEX grew 9% QoQ  on
salary adjustment and higher bonus accrual. Non-op gain of NT$266mn mainly includes
valuation gains on an investee company. For Q426F, it guided sales  flat  QoQ remaining
at high levels with IP and NRE up QoQ, and turnkey down QoQ. GM is guided back to
~45%, reflecting normalized ASIC economics; OPEX should decline single digit QoQ.
2027 growth outlook supported by AI, advanced packaging and 12nm node

Management guided for double-digit sales growth in 2027, with all three businesses, IP ,
NRE and turnkey, expected to grow, driven by three key catalysts: AI, advanced
packaging and new UMC 12nm project contribution. For IP , growth should be
supported by higher royalties on foundry price hikes/customer capacity expansion and
initial  contribution from 12nm node; NRE should benefit from 2nm/4nm projects
entering peak recognition, with 3-4 ASIC tape-outs targeted in H127; while MP should

see growth in both MCU and ASIC as new projects gradually ramp. AI-related mix was at
8-9% of Q326 sales, and is expected to increase throughout 2027 alongside
accelerating advanced-packaging shipments. We expect 2027E sales to grow 37% YoY.
2nm design win opens new advanced-node and product opportunities
Faraday secured a 2nm GAA special purpose  ASIC project worth several tens of millions
of US dollars in NRE, which management described as its largest-ever design win and

most advanced project to date. The project spans front-end design and IP integration,
back-end design, customized 3D DRAM and advanced packaging, and marks Faraday's
first customized 3D DRAM and hybrid-bonding project. It expects to recognize its first
NRE milestone  in H127, followed by MP targeted within 1-2 years. Beyond 2nm, Faraday
is expanding into new applications including OCS, CXL and Si-cap, leveraging its
capabilities in advanced packaging, interposer design and ASIC integration. We believe

successful execution by Faraday and its foundry partner could strengthen Faraday's
positioning in advanced-node projects and create follow-on design-win opportunities.
Valuation: Raise PT to NT$200, stay Neutral
We raise    2026E EPS (reported) from NT$3.43 to NT$3.68 on better Q326, but lower
2027/28E EPS by 4%/1% to factor in softer CE demand and higher OPEX. We raise our
PT to NT$200 from NT$190 using 24x 2027E PE (vs prior 22x). We raise our multiple as

the recent 2nm project win should boost investor confidence, but stay Neutral as the
advanced nodes (4nm/2nm) turnkey revenue recognition depends on the foundry
partner's execution. We await clearer evidence before revisiting our assumptions.

Company Description
Faraday was founded in 1993, spun off from UMC's IP and semiconductor design service
department as the first IC design service company in Asia. It is an IC design and production
service provider that initially focused on specialty applications on mature nodes by partnering

with UMC and gradually upgraded to advanced nodes through working with Samsung. The
company had its IPO on the Taiwan Securities Exchange in 1999.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value Faraday using PE methodology.
Downside risks include slower-than-expected advanced nodes migration, further delay of
advanced packaging ramp, and growing competition in specialty projects from China IC
design service companies.

months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA)  is the one-year local

by the analyst to indicate that the stock's price target and/or rating may change in the near term, usually in response to an event that

may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have a 12-month investment horizon.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Update
Accton Technology Corporation  | Asia Pacific
Read-across from Celestica's
3Q26 Results
We see positive implications for Accton given demand strength
(1.6T migration amid sustaining 800G) and breadth (scale up
opportunities) of ethernet  switches in future.
Celestica (CLS.N, not covered) 3Q26 results:
• 3Q26 revenue of US$5,830mn (+24% QoQ/+83% YoY), non-GAAP operating
margin of 8.5%,  a record high, and non-GAAP EPS of US$3.30 (+30% QoQ/

+109% YoY).
° Higher than guidance and 7%/13% above consensus for revenue/earnings,
respectively.
° Drivers: Stronger CCS revenue (84% of the mix in 3Q26), including
networking and compute/storage growth of 68% and 242% YoY,
respectively, vs. guidance 60% and 190% YoY.
• 4Q26 revenue guided at US$6,275-6,575mn (+10% QoQ/+76% YoY at the
midpoint) vs. consensus at US$6,396mn, management raising 2026 target to
US$21bn for revenue (+69% YoY vs. prior US$20.5bn) and US$11.55 for non-

GAAP EPS  (+91% YoY vs. prior US$11.3).
° Driven by hyperscaler-led 800G demand and ramping 1.6T programs,
along with continued AI compute and storage demand.
• 2027 revenue guided at US$40bn (+90% YoY), non-GAAP OPM at 9.0%
(+0.6ppt YoY) and non-GAAP EPS at US$21.5 (+86% YoY)
° It expects CCS to grow 110% (1.6T/800G on networking and ASIC ramp
on compute/storage) and  ATS at 10%, with revenue skewed to  2H on
multiple large ramps and momentum likely sustaining into  2028.

• Pluggable should remain the mainstream interconnect solution, while CPO
might pick up more meaningfully from 3.2T, per company.
• CLS expects ethernet scale up could represent new opportunities in future.
Our view:
• While the customer profiles are different, we still see positive implications
from Celestica's comments for Accton regarding demand strength (1.6T
migration amid sustaining 800G) and breadth (scale up opportunities) of
ethernet  switches.

• We stay constructive on Accton, and believe   it will continue to benefit from
the secular growth in network switches amid increasing data traffic, spec
upgrades, and a wider customer base as well as the AI accelerator modules
on the initiatives for compute diversity.

More Takeaways
Further comments from Celestica:
• Demand visibility is lengthening and it is now having conversations with customers
for 2029/2030 projects
• Still seeing lots of pluggables during 1.6T, while it expects CPO to play out more
prominently in 3.2T.
• It has won a new hyperscaler program to co-design and manufacture a 1.6T rack-
scale CPO networking solution, with production scheduled to ramp in late 2027,

per management.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Accton Technology Corporation (2345.TW)
Base case, P/E, which we believe better captures the company's earnings growth momentum.
We assign a target P/E of 30x to our 2027 EPS estimate, which we view as justified by an
operating profit CAGR of 48% for 2025-28, based on our estimates. This implies a PEG of
around 0.6, vs. its average of 0.7 over 2019-25, when the stock was trading at a P/E of 22x for
an operating profit CAGR of 32%.

Risks to Upside
n Stronger data center capex
n Faster network switch spec migration
n InfiniBand losing share in AI networks
n Stronger custom AI accelerator growth
n Stronger ODM direct business model adoption
Risks to Downside
n Weaker data center capex
n Slower network switch spec migration
n InfiniBand taking more share in AI networks
n Softer custom AI accelerator growth
n Softer ODM direct business model adoption

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

研 究  報 告                
力成(6239.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
4Q26 營收維持高檔，關注高階封裝業務布局進度  
 投資評價與建議 
我們認為高階封裝業務進度如期，且為明年公司重點布局業務，整體狀況與原
先預期差異不大；我們給予公司18倍PE，為近期股價修正前之平均評價水準。 
 重點評論分析 
估4Q26營收維持高檔：當前公司封裝、測試產能利用率均達90%以上，推估
營收動能包含：(1)記憶體需求維持高檔，新業務TSV DDR5開始量產，另公司
持續因應記憶體報價上漲 ， 適時反映於封測報價 ； (2)邏輯業務中 ， FCBGA需求
維持穩健且公司將於本季導入更大尺寸封裝 ， 子公司超豐Flip Chip月產能於年
底擴充至2.3億顆，至於Tera Probe/Terapower除了有AI/MML需求支撐外，
⾞用需求回溫亦帶來成⻑動能。估4Q26營收季增5.8%至268億元，毛利率
24.1%，稅後EPS 3.8元。 
高階封裝業務逐步發酵、封測報價議價能力強勁支撐2027年成⻑動能 ：展望
2027年，基於：(1)DRAM市場供不應求至少延續至 2027年，機構估2027年
DRAM報價續漲但漲幅收斂，推估將持續支撐漲價成本之能力；(2)邏輯業務
中，封測產業產能利用率維持高檔，持續支撐公司議價能力，而公司高階封裝
比重成⻑帶動產品組合持續轉佳，如FOPLP明年有望開出3k月產能，子公司
Tera Probe/Terapower持續搶攻AI測試業務，另Terapower新竹新廠將於明
年貢獻營收。預估2027年營收年增22.5%至1,182.4億元，稅後EPS 18元。 
 投資風險 
FOPLP進展不如預期。

| 營運概況  
3Q26本業表現大致符合預期：力成3Q26營收季增9.6%至253.3億元，毛利率24%，稅後EPS 3.44元，其中本業表
現大致符合預期，營收動能來自於記憶體需求維持穩健，子公司超豐價格調漲和客⼾需求成⻑，T e r a  
Probe/Terapower持續受惠AI/MML、HPC封測訂單支撐，而獲利方面則有價格調漲、高產能利用率(力成3Q26封
裝、測試產能利用率分別為85~90%、85%)之挹注，帶動毛利率季增2.3ppts。 
估4Q26營收維持高檔：當前公司封裝、測試產能利用率均達90%以上，推估4Q26營收動能包含：(1)記憶體業務需
求維持高檔，其中企業級SSD持續受惠於AI伺服器需求，另TSV DDR5開始量產，估月營收貢獻約1~2億元，價格
方面，公司持續因應記憶體報價上漲並適時反映於封測報價；(2)邏輯業務中，FOPLP驗證進度如期推進，FCBGA
需求維持穩健且公司將於本季導入更大尺寸封裝(78mm*88mm)，子公司超豐Flip Chip月產能於年底擴充至2.3億
顆 ， 另將部分不具成本優勢之產品轉移至菲律賓廠量產 ， 至於Tera Probe/Terapower除了有AI/MML需求支撐外 ，
⾞用需求回溫亦帶來成⻑動能。我們估4Q26營收季增5.8%至268億元，毛利率24.1%，稅後EPS 3.8元。 
高階封裝業務逐步發酵、封測報價議價能力強勁支撐2027年成⻑動能：展望2027年，基於：(1)DRAM市場供不應
求至少延續至2027年，機構估2027年DRAM報價續漲但漲幅收斂，推估將持續支撐漲價成本之能力，至於NAND
市況據機構預估明年仍有個位數的缺口或趨於平衡，僅有企業級SSD需求仍維持強勁；(2)邏輯業務則為明年最大成
⻑動能，基於整體產業產能利用率維持高檔，支撐公司議價能力，加上高階封裝比重成⻑帶動產品組合持續轉佳，
包含FOPLP明年有望開出3k月產能，推估應用為AMD之電競晶片，子公司Tera Probe/Terapower除了既有業務持
續成⻑外，也積極搶攻美系客⼾下⼀代AI ASIC測試商機，另Terapower新竹新廠也將於明年開始貢獻營收(估月營
收貢獻約2~3億元)。預估2027年營收年增22.5%至1,182.4億元，毛利率24.5%，稅後EPS 18元。

## Document 170: 6467泰合 永豐

Source PDF: all report pdf/6467泰合 永豐.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

個股聚焦  
2026/10/20

陳奕均 fion.chen@sinopac.com

泰合 (6467 TT) 
開花結果在即

永豐觀點 
抗血栓口溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即。 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議買進：泰合 10/20 上市掛牌，掛牌價105.6 元。由於(1) 泰合開發之口溶
膜劑型藥物有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點下，市場競爭力足；(2) 抗血栓口
溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即，2027 年在授權與上市權利金開始認列下 ，營運轉
虧為盈且呈大跳躍狀；(3) 由於多數學名藥廠受限專利訴訟，最快需至 2028 年 4
月後才可上市銷售，促使泰合享有約一年的「黃金市場空窗期」 ，能與原廠直接瓜
分市場。且因 BEQIA 屬 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥，即便2Q28 後學名藥陸續進場，其
獨特的劑型優勢仍能築起價格防護牆，所面臨的價折壓力相對輕微，建議買進，目
標價 170 元( 18 X 2027 EPS )。

營運現況與分析 
公司簡介：泰合生技成立於 2010 年，專注開發 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥與特殊學名
藥，核心技術為自主研發的 TDS 透皮／透黏膜傳輸平台，將現有口服藥品成分改
造為透皮與透黏膜藥物傳導劑型 ，提供方便使用且更安全的新劑型藥物。 產品開發
上，505(b)(2)部分，治療抗血栓口溶膜貼片(每日兩片)BEQIA 靜待歐美取證中外，
其餘則仍在臨床階段中，困難學名藥部分，化療止吐口溶膜2018 年日本取證銷售
中外，其餘四項藥品則在進行洽談授權合作。 
圖一：泰合產品線開發時程

資料來源：泰合；永豐投顧研究部整理，Oct. 2026

### Page 2

2

口溶膜劑型藥物能有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點 ：傳統口服錠劑藥物必須經
過食道、進入胃腸道消化，再經由肝臟代謝後才進入血液循環，泰合開發之口溶膜
劑型藥物， 可讓藥膜在接觸舌頭或口腔黏膜數秒內快速溶解 ， 病患完全不需飲水即
可完成給藥， 且部份藥物分子能直接透過口腔黏膜上皮細胞微血管吸收， 直接進入
全身血液循環， 不僅能提高藥物生體可用率， 還能減少胃酸對藥物的破壞與對胃腸
道的刺激下，有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點，提高患者服藥順從性。 
抗血栓口溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即，提前卡位下，營運動能可期：泰合自主
開發的抗血栓口溶膜新劑型新藥 BEQIA， 預計4Q26 取得歐盟藥證 、2Q27 獲美國
FDA 核准上市。目前該藥原廠在美國市場年銷售額>200 億美元，相較原廠口服錠
劑，泰合 BEQIA 具備三大競爭優勢：不需飲水（服藥便利）、解決吞嚥困難（減
輕照顧端壓力），以及攜帶與給藥便利（優化長照與醫療機構給藥流程）。 
市場競爭方面，由於多數學名藥廠受限專利訴訟，最快需至2028 年 4 月後才可上
市銷售，促使泰合享有約一年的「黃金市場空窗期」 ，能與原廠直接瓜分市場。此
外，由於BEQIA 屬 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥，即便2Q28 後學名藥陸續進場，其獨特
的劑型優勢仍能築起價格防護牆，所面臨的折價壓力應相對輕微。 
泰合海外市場營銷預計將採授權分潤模式，研究部預估 1H27 歐美地區簽約金應
有機會各達 500~1000 萬美金外，隨美國地區 2Q27 上市、通路鋪設及醫保陸續
准入，Peak sales 市佔率 2-3%(預期將落於 2029-30 年)、銷售分潤 12-15%估算
下，稅後 EPS 貢獻將落於 10-20 元。 
財務預估：預估泰合 2026 年營收 0.1 億元，稅後虧損 1.89 億元，稅後 EPS 以掛
牌後股本 5.99 億元估算，稅後 EPS-3.15 元，2027 年預估歐美地區授權簽約金各
500 萬美金及 1000 萬美金 ， 及上市銷售後權利金認列下 ，預估營收將跳升至7.75
億元，稅後淨利 5.68 億元，稅後 EPS 9.49 元。

## Document 188: [000270] 기아(000270) 물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결기아(000270) 물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결 - 기업

주가 추이

견조한 판매 확대로 매출 견조, 이익은 시장 기대치 하회 예상 
3Q26 동사 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 33.6조원(+17.3% 이하 Y oY)과 2.39조
원(+63.6%) 기록 전망. 3Q 매출액은 친환경차 중심 신차 확대 통해 두 자릿수 
성장세를 기록. 3Q 판매량은 도매 기준 84.3만대(+7.6% 이하 Y oY)로 전년 동
기 대비 성장 폭을 확대했고, 특히 친환경차 판매량 29.8만대(+45.9%)를 기록
하며 이번 분기 매출 성장에 절대적으로 기여.  
다만, 3Q 수익성은 4~5월 상승했던 원자재 투입 영향과 ‘경제형EV 믹스’가 더
욱 확대(13.2% → 14.5%)되면서 단기 수익성에 부정적 작용하며 시장 기대치 
2.5조원을 다소 하회할 전망. 그러나, 기말 환율이 1,356원/$ 수준으로 낮 아 지
면서 전년 동기 수 익 성 악화에 크게 영 향 을 미쳤던 판매보증비 외화환산손익 
영향이 개선(1.6%p 수준) 기대되며, 관세 부담 또한 북미 HEV 확대로 전년 동
기 대비 약 4,500억원(2.0%p) 감소하면서 부정적 수익성 영향 만회 전망.  
유럽은 점유율 확대, 북미는 수익성 개선으로 
올해 북미 HEV와 유럽 BEV 중심 신차 출시 및 물량 확대가 본격화되면
서, ’27년에는 이익 성장 기여도가 확대될 것으로 전망. 미국 조 지 아 텔 루 라 이
드 HEV 신규 생산(2.8만대) 및 HMGMA 스 포 티 지HEV신규 생산(1.0만
대) 등 수 요 가 높은 북미 현 지 에 서 HEV 물량 확대 중으로 4Q에도 텔루라이
드 HEV와 스포티지 HEV의 현지 생산/판매 확대를 비롯하여 셀토스 HEV 
출시로 ’27년 북미 중심 HEV 신차 물량 증가 및 믹스 개선 기대. 유럽은 강한 
BEV 수요가 지속되면서 중국 업체들의 공세 강화 중이나 동사의 물량 확대 또
한 견조. 3Q EV3 2.9만대, EV5 2.2만대 등 유럽 BEV 수요 대응 전략 주효. ’27
년에는 K4/셀토스 및 쏘렌토 등 HEV 신차 중 심 의 친환경차 PT 믹스 다양화
로 유럽 내 물량 확대 흐름 지속 전망 
물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결, 기말 배당 매력도 부각 
’27년 동사 영업이익 추 정 치 는 11.1조원(+13.5%)으로 증익 지속 전망. 최근 
환율 추세 고려 시 내년 수익성에 부 정 적 영향 확대 가능성 존재하나, 올해 확
인된 신차 중심 물량 확대를 통해 부정적 환율 영향을 상쇄할 것 으 로 기대. 
12M EPS 추정치 하향(-2.9%) 및 상 반 기 적 용 한 코스피 프리미엄 제거(-
10%)로 목 표 주 가 를 260,000원으로 하향. 그러나, RA 설립에서의 동사 지분 
참여 및 기말 배당(’26년 DPS 7,000원 추정)에 따른 상 대 적 배당 수익 매력
도 부각 등은 주가 모 멘 텀 에 있어 기대 가능한 요소.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10월 8 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 8

기아 (000270) [한화리서치]

8 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

Source PDF: all report pdf/[012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에 재진입한화에어로스페이스(012450) 일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에_b94e5eb0.pdf

### Page 1

한화에어로스페이스 (012450/KS) 
일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에 재진입 
2026-10-30

조선/방산. 한승한, RA. 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 1,300,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 760,000 원 
상승여력: 71.1%

Signal: 3Q26, 수출 물량 저조 및 국내 낮은 마진 물량 인식으로 어닝 쇼크 
Key: 4Q부터 내년까지 수출 물량 빠르게 회복하며 실적 성장 이어갈 전망 
Step: 연내 새로운 중장기 성장동력이 될 중동 수주계약 기대. 매수 기회

3Q26 Review: 수출 물량 납품 저조로 어닝 쇼크  
3Q26 연결 매출액 6 조 943 억원(-6.0% YoY, -34.4% QoQ), 영업이익 4,520억원
(-46.2% YoY, -66.9% QoQ)으로, 시장예상치(1조 1,615억원)를 대폭 하회하는 어닝 쇼
크 기록했다. ①폴란드 중심의 수출 물량의 매출 인식 시점이 4 분기로 이연되며, 수출 물량 
비중이 감소한 점이 컸다. ②내수 물량 또한 감소했으며, 특히 국내 물량 중에서도 상대적
으로 마진율 낮은 유무인 무기체계 초도 물량이 이번 분기에 집중됐다. ③내수&수출 전반
적인 지상방산 매출 비중 감소에 따른 고정비 부담이 컸으며, ④일회성 요인으로 대전 사업
장 사고에 따른 복구 비용 약 300 억원과 충당금 및 기타 비용 총 160 억원, 그리고 환율 
하락으로 영업이익의 약 10% 수준의 감소 효과 발생했다. 다만 4Q26 부터 이번 분기 이
연된 폴란드 중심의 수출 물량 인식이 반영되면서 큰 폭의 탑라인 및 이익 성장 시현할 전
망이며, 대전 사업장 또한 내년 상반기 가동 재개 예상됨에 따라 지상방산 부문은 전년 대비 
성장 폭 두드러지며 사측 가이던스 15% 매출 성장 충분히 달성 가능할 전망이다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 130 만원으로 하향 
한화에어로스페이스에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가 기존 160 만원에서 130
만원으로 하향한다. 최근 Peer 그룹의 주가 하락에 따라 멀티플 하향 조정했기 때문이다. 
폴란드 천무 유도탄 JV에 이어 현지화가 전제된 K9 3차 계약을 추진 중이며, 국내 장약 생
산능력 증설을 통한 유럽향 수요 대응과 미국 내 생산거점 구축까지 진행 중에 있다. 또한 
미국 차륜형 자주포 사업 시제 선정에 이은 궤도형 사업 수주까지 기대할 수 있다. 중동 국
가들의 방공무기체계에 대한 계약 논의는 매우 가시적인 상황이며, 사우디 MNG 사업 수주 
또한 시점의 문제일 뿐이다. 기존 유럽에 더해 중동은 현지화 전략을 기반으로 동사의 새로
운 중장기 성장동력이 될 지역이며, 올해 4 분기부터 해당 수주 모멘텀과 함께 큰 폭의 실적 
성장이 맞물리며 리레이팅 가능할 전망이다. 선제적 매수 기회라는 판단이다.

## Document 359: Our first impression on Anthropic’s income statement in IPO prospectus

Source PDF: all report pdf/Our first impression on Anthropic’s income statement in IPO prospectus.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-10-27
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    陸⾏之  Andrew Lu on global semis and techs <andrewhclu@substack.com>
Subject: Our first impression on Anthropic’s income statement in IPO prospectus
Date:    9 minutes ago
Our first impression on Anthropic’s income statement in IPO prospectus
View in browser
Our ﬁrst impression on
Anthropic’s income statement
in IPO prospectus
從  Anthropic IPO 招股說明書財務報表，我們看到了公司 10 倍速的
增⻑，超分散的客戶結構，近 50GW 算⼒合約及兩項驚⼈的⾮現⾦費
⽤
ANDREW LU ON GLOBAL SEMI/TECHS
OCT 27 ∙ PAID

READ IN APP
Executive summary
Anthropic 跟  OpenAI 兩⼤⼤語⾔模型⿓頭應該是今年及明年美國 IPO 的重頭
戲，也是⽬前全球 AI 數據算⼒中⼼基礎建設及產業鏈最主要的兩⼤客戶，這兩
家公司的營收增⻑，獲利變化，算⼒租⽤契約都跟產業鏈息息相關，⽬前看來
Anthropic 3Q26 營收季增確實有趨緩現象，但 3Q26 及 2026 營收年增依然有
10 倍以上增速，值得關注的是不同於 AI 數據算⼒中⼼如 AWS, ⾕歌  Cloud, 微
軟  Intelligent Cloud 及新興雲  SpaceXSI, Oracle Cloud, CoreWeave,
Nebius 僅靠著少數⼤語⾔如 Anthropic, OpenAI, Meta, 微軟及⾕歌等⼤語
⾔模型公司的基礎建設積壓訂單，看起來產業鏈⾵險極⾼，但⿓頭  Anthropic
及其他同業卻有著⾮常健康分散的客戶結構。⾄於未來 10 年近 50GW 算⼒合約
（ AI 數據算⼒中⼼的積壓訂單）是否能執⾏？及如果把⾮現⾦項⽬如可轉換票

據，衍⽣性負債算⼊ GAAP 費⽤， Anthropic 的短期季度虧損似乎將隨著負債
及估值擴⼤⽽持續放⼤，那何時將結束呢？

### Page 2

Source: Anthropic
10x yoy growth in sales
Anthropic 近 8 成營收從  Pay-for-intelligence 來的，其中包括賣 tokens 及模
型使⽤收⼊，剩下的 2 成才是訂閱費⽤， 1Q26 及  2Q26 營收季增仍各有
132% 及 144% ，年增各有 1158% 及 1368% 。但  Anthropic 在提交  S-1 的
同時，向參與  Roadshow 的投⾏與⼤型機構投資⼈另⾏披露的未經審計最新
季度（ 3Q26 ）經營數據與營收指引達 168 億美元，季增 45% ，年增降低⾄
1116% 。該數據反映的是  7 到  9 ⽉的單季表現，得益於  Claude Code 與新
模型的  Token 調⽤量激增，單季  168 億美元幾乎等同於整個上半年的總
和）。按照  SEC 慣例，待  11 ⽉中旬路演正式定價及遞交補充修訂案（ S-1/A ）
時， Q3 的完整財報才會正式併⼊法定報表附註中。
Customers

Anthropic 的主要客戶包括 AI 新創，研究機構，各⼤企業。公司並公布到
2026 年 9 ⽉底為⽌，指在過去這  12 個⽉內（ TTM ， Trailing Twelve
Months 從  2025 年  10 ⽉  1 ⽇⾄  2026 年  9 ⽉  30 ⽇  這整整  12 個⽉期
間），累計為公司貢獻營收達到或超過  10 萬美元  TTM 企業客戶，這類客戶從
2025 年  9 ⽉底的  950 多家，增加到 2025 年  12 ⽉底的 1,500 家，然後暴增
⾄  2026 年  9 ⽉底的  11,900 多家，因為客戶相當分散，⽬前只有⼀家客戶
營收超過  Anthropic 總營收的  10% ，這其中超過 1000 萬美元的客戶有 250
家，⽽超過 100 萬美元的客戶有 1,900 家。

義務開始⽣效（合計年均 922 億美元算⼒合約，約每年 4-5GW 算⼒增量於

2030 年， 2-3GW 於 2026-2029 ， 6-7GW 於 2031-2035 ， 10 年合計約 45-
50GW 算⼒），這些費⽤亦納⼊了營業租賃成本、融資租賃資產攤銷、變動租
賃成本，以及⾃有伺服器、網路設備與相關資料中⼼資產的折舊與攤銷。
Anthropic 將於  April 2026-July 2033 租⽤⾕歌  1,145 億美元算⼒（ 7
1/3 年⻑約，年均 156 億美元算⼒合約），並於 2028-2031 年採購近
1250-1500 億美元的 TPUs ；
Anthropic 透過融資（透過發可轉換票據給博通）租賃合約使⽤博通建⽴
的⾕歌 TPUs 及機櫃算⼒，總計合約達 1,612 億美元  (5 年⻑約，年均 322
億美元算⼒合約）；
Anthropic 租⽤亞⾺遜  AWS 的數據算⼒中⼼，從 2026 年 5 ⽉到 2036 年
4 ⽉近 10 年，合約總計達 1,100 億美元（ 10 年⻑約，年均 110 億美元算⼒
合約）；

Anthropic 租⽤ SpaceXSI 的算⼒中⼼  Colossus I/II 使⽤ Nvidia GPU
集群，從 2026 年 5 ⽉份⾄ 2029 年，開始每⽉付出 12.5 億美元，逐步增加

美元算⼒（ 6.5 年⻑約，年均 69 億美元算⼒合約）；
Anthropic 將於  2027 年採購近 200 億美元  AMD MI455 Helios AI 算⼒
機櫃；
Anthropic 將於  Aug 2026-2033 租⽤ Nscale Global 近 446 億美元算
⼒（ 7 5/12 年⻑約，年均 60 億美元算⼒合約）；
Anthropic 將於  2026-2033 租⽤ Lambda 近 413 億美元算⼒（ 7.5 年⻑

約，年均 55 億美元算⼒合約）。
算⼒是 Anthropic 最主要的營運成本，⽽算⼒效率則是決定公司營運效率與營
收的關鍵要素。由研究驅動的演算法改進（涵蓋訓練與推論優化），結合多晶
⽚算⼒架構，在營收規模擴張的同時，⼤幅提升了算⼒效率。這使得公司在每
投⼊⼀美元算⼒投資時，所能產⽣的模型智能⽔準以及創造的業務營收均能持
續提升。這是為何我們預估 Anthropic 隨著營收在 2H26-2030 的持續增⻑，
Anthropic 的營業利潤率及將股權激勵⾮現⾦費⽤加回的調整後營業利潤率應
該有持續改善的空間。
訓練效率（ Training Eﬃciency ）
Anthropic 的研究⼈員持續研發出具乘數效應的算⼒優化技術。更重要的是，
Claude 本⾝已越來越深⼊協助研發與⼯程⼯作，以打造後續各代模型版本；
公司稱這種遞迴動態為閉環（ closing the loop ），由新⼀代模型加速下⼀代
模型的研發，使這些效益在模型世代交替間產⽣複合式增⻑。
推論效率（ Inference Eﬃciency ）

研究團隊在推論端實現了顯著的算⼒乘數效益 —— ⼤幅降低在提供特定模型效
能表現時所需的算⼒；同時，⼯程團隊也⼤幅優化了⽣產環境中算⼒資源的利
⽤率。這使得公司能為客戶提供更⾼的價值，並隨著時間推移從現有算⼒基礎
上創造更⾼的營收。
多晶⽚算⼒架構（ Multi-Chip Compute Approach ）
Anthropic 透過專屬的編排層（ orchestration layer ），使  Claude 能原⽣在
四⼤主流晶⽚系列上進⾏訓練與推論服務： AWS Trainium 、 Google TPU 、
NVIDIA GPU 以及  AMD GPU ，這讓公司能針對價格與效能最佳化各項⼯作負
載。這項具備⾼度彈性的架構歷時多年與巨額投資打造。它不僅擴⼤了可獲得
的算⼒供應、降低對單⼀供應商的依賴，更能讓公司持續將不同⼯作負載指派
⾄最適⽤的硬體上，將適配新晶⽚平台所需的時間⼤幅縮短。

Personnel expenses
⼈事費⽤主要是由近  7 成的股權激勵費⽤（ Stock-based Compensation ）
所組成，外加現⾦薪酬，及其他福利與税費，這其中包含三⼤  non-GAAP 的
股票選擇權（ Stock Options ）、限制性股票單位（ Restricted stock unit,
RSUs ）及限制性股票獎勵（ Restricted stock awards, RSAs ）。  我們認為
這次  IPO 上市可能將帶來的⼀次性  SBC 費⽤激增，尤其是部分原先附帶上市 /
績效條件（ Performance Condition ）的特定  RSUs 將直接達成歸屬條件，
因此在該招股說明書⽣效時，將認列⼀筆額外且顯著的股權激勵費⽤，但因為

Source: Anthropic
Change in fair value of convertible notes,
derivative liabilities, and SAFEs
這些⾮經常性費⽤的本質是可轉債與衍⽣⼯具內含「看漲期權（ Call Option ）
的費⽤。
投資⼈持有的權利：當  Amazon 、 Google 等機構購買  Anthropic 的可轉換
票據（ Convertible Notes ）或簽訂未來認購協議（衍⽣性負債）時，他們不
只擁有⼀筆借給公司的債券本⾦，更同時擁有在未來以約定價格（通常附帶折
扣或估值上限  Cap ）將本⾦轉換成公司股票的權利。
期權價值隨估值暴增：當  Anthropic 的企業估值從幾百億美元⼀路飆升⾄幾
千億甚⾄上兆美元時，投資⼈⼿中這張「低價換股憑證」的潛在增值利益極其
龐⼤。對投資⼈（資產⽅）⽽⾔，這筆投資的公允價值暴增；但對發⾏⽅

Anthropic （負債⽅）⽽⾔，這代表公司未來必須交付價值遠⾼於當初借款⾦
額的股權。
可以從下⽅圖表看出來， Anthropic GAAP 財報的主要虧損就是因為這可轉換
票據及衍⽣性負債因公司估值及股價增加⽽擴⼤虧損，但因為這並⾮現⾦項
⽬，所以在調整後並不會影響整體獲利及營運現⾦流。⼀旦公司完成  IPO ，這
些可轉債與衍⽣協議將全數債轉股（轉換為特別股或普通股），屆時這筆⾦融
負債會直接轉移⾄股東權益（ Equity ）項下，未來便不再需要每季按公允價
值重估，也不會再持續侵蝕損益表。
⼀、會計處理選擇：採⽤「公允價值選擇權（ Fair Value Option ）」
會計準則依據：依據美國會計準則  ASC 825 （⾦融⼯具），公司選擇按「公允
價值（ Fair Value ）」計量發⾏的可轉換票據（ Convertible Notes ）。

### Page 7

資產負債表與損益表認列：可轉債以估計的公允價值列於資產負債表。⽽每季
度公允價值的變動，直接認列於合併損益表中的可轉換本票、衍⽣性負債及
SAFE 公允價值變動」科⽬。
選擇動機：消除傳統拆分模式（ Bifurcation Model ）下將「負債」與「權益」
分別獨⽴核算的複雜性。
⼆、 Amazon 與  Google 協議所衍⽣之「衍⽣性負債（ Derivative
Liabilities ）」
針對  2023 年與  2024 年與  Amazon 及  Google 簽訂的本票購買協議
（ Note Purchase Agreements ），公司認列了代表「依約有義務在未來發⾏
新可轉債」的衍⽣性負債。這些衍⽣性負債同樣以預估公允價值計量，每期的
估值波動亦全數計⼊同⼀損益表科⽬。

agreements
三、估值的⾼度主觀性與關鍵假設變數
該估值極度依賴複雜且主觀的假設，任何變更都會對損益表認列⾦額產⽣重⼤

衝擊（ Material Impact ）：
結算情境發⽣的機率與時間點：例如合格股權融資、 IPO 、直接上市（ Direct
Listing ）或併購 / 控制權變更的時程。
底層標的股權的公允價值：即公司整體估值的暴漲或暴跌。
動態轉換價格與折價幅度：依不同輪次票據條款、發⾏經過時間、估值上限
（ Cap ）與折扣率（ Discount ）計算。

### Page 8

持股上限與展期機制（ Ownership Cap & Rollover Mechanism ）：
條款規定換股⽐例設有上限，超出上限的未轉換本⾦將重新發⾏成「不計息的
展期票據（ Rollover Note ）」，且該展期票據仍需持續進⾏公允價值衡量。由
於未來融資規模與結構具⾼度不確定性，需深度主觀判斷有多少⽐例會「轉成
股權」或「轉為展期票據」。
四、 SAFE （未來股權簡單協議）的處理現況
2023 年發⾏的  SAFE 同樣採⽤上述情境公允價值模型計量。
現狀清空：所有  SAFE 已於  2023 與  2024 年度全數轉換為可贖回可轉換特
別股。
截⾄  2024 年底、 2025 年底及  2026 年上半年（截⾄  6 ⽉  30 ⽇），帳上已
無任何未結清的  SAFE 。未來持續⾯臨公允價值波動與估值⾵險的核⼼部位，
已全部聚焦在上述可轉換本票（ Convertible Notes ）及其展期票據
（ Rollover Notes ）。

## Document 371: Regional Roundup_ Coast-to-coast growth From spending to wages, growth is broad-based across the US, though the South is the clear leader in new business applications. 2 min read

Source PDF: all report pdf/Regional Roundup_ Coast-to-coast growth From spending to wages, growth is broad-based across the US, though the South is the clear leader in new business applications. 2 min read.pdf
Report source: Bank of America
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

13035502     1

Economy 
Regional Roundup: Coast-to-coast growth 
29 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• Bank of America aggregated credit and debit card spending per household is growing at healthy rates across the country. 
Discretionary spending growth has improved markedly relative to the last two years. While the West has led recently and the 
South has lagged somewhat, no region appears to be experiencing a meaningful consumer slowdown. 
• The South has seen softer discretionary retail spending, particularly in home-related durable goods categories. However, the 
region continues to post the strongest after-tax wage growth and has been the largest contributor to growth in new business 
applications nationwide. 
• Business formation is occurring across a wide range of industries, with professional services, retail, healthcare, construction and 
hospitality all accounting for meaningful shares of new business applications. While entrepreneurial activity is broad-based 
across industries nationwide, industry revenue mixes vary considerably by region.

Spending growth is solid across all regions  
Spending growth was strongest in the West in September, while spending growth lagged in the Northeast and South, though not 
by much. Total credit and debit card spending per household increased around 5.6% year-over-year (YoY) in the West, outpacing 
other regions by less than a percentage point, according to Bank of America aggregated card data (Exhibit 1). All regions also 
experienced major improvements in spending growth compared to last year, increasing by over three percentage points in every 
region.   
Spending growth in the Northeast peaked at around 6% YoY in June but remained a solid 4.8% YoY in September. The slowest 
growth was in the South, though spending was still up nearly 4.8% YoY.  
Exhibit 1: Spending growth has mostly improved since the start of the year across all regions 
Total card spending per household by US Census region (three-month moving average, YoY%)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Discretionary spending growth was also somewhat weaker in the Northeast and South than in the Midwest and West (Exhibit 2). 
However, these two regions have also seen the most improvement in “nice-to-have” spending over the past two years. And here 
spending was also up around 4.8% YoY in both regions, still a very healthy rate of growth.

Durables spending is weighing on discretionary retail spending in the South 
One of the key themes from September in the overall spending data was a broadening in spending growth, particularly in “more 
discretionary” retail categories like clothing, electronics, building materials and non-store/miscellaneous retailers (read more in 
Consumer Checkpoint: Strength in numbers). Bank of America card data shows that this improvement is occurring across all 
regions.  
However, the South lags, though this is mostly due to declines in spending on home-related durables in, for instance, home 
improvement stores and at furniture retailers. In our view, this could reflect some slowdown in domestic migration to the Sun 
Belt and a corresponding easing in home/moving-related spending (read more in On the move: Renovation over relocation?) 
Exhibit 3: The Midwest and Northeast have seen a higher share of discretionary retail categories with spending growth when compared to the 
South and West 
Diffusion index across “more discretionary” retail categories by spending across US Census regions (monthly, %)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

The South leads in after-tax wage and business application growth 
One reason we are not overly concerned about consumer spending in the South is the strength of after-tax wage growth in the 
region. Bank of America deposit data suggests that after-tax wage growth was strongest there, though only by a slim margin, up 
nearly 4.2% YoY, just ahead of households in the Midwest, and outpacing the West and Northeast by less than half a percentage 
point (Exhibit 4).

Beyond wage income, business ownership offers another potential channel for wealth accumulation. The South has been the 
primary contributor to overall US growth in new business applications so far in 2026, according to US Census Bureau data 
(Exhibit 5).  
While historically only a small share of these applications is likely to become a business employing workers, this surge in 
applications points to a potential pipeline of entrepreneurial activity. For businesses that become established and profitable, 
ownership can provide a pathway to wealth through retained earnings and the accumulation of business equity. Still, the extent 
of that opportunity depends on businesses’ survival, profitability and financing needs (read more in Business Checkpoint: Looking 
into logistics).

though growth was strongest in the South and Midwest

BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Business application growth is widespread across regions and industries 
Looking at 2026 business applications by industry, entrepreneurial activity spans multiple industry groupings (Exhibit 6). 
Professional, scientific, technical and other services account for the largest share, followed by retail trade and the grouping 
comprising healthcare, education services and the arts. Additionally, businesses provide a significant source of income for US 
households. In fact, business revenues accounted for nearly 14% of total pre-tax income, according to the US Census’ 2025 
Survey of Consumer Finances.

Combined, this suggests that potential pathways to business ownership extend not only across various households (read more on 
generational shifts in entrepreneurship in July’s A new generation of business formation) but also across many industries, rather 
than a single sector. 
While business applications show the breadth of prospective entrepreneurial activity nationwide, the industry composition of 
existing business revenue offers a regional lens on business activity. Exhibit 7 shows how revenue was distributed across 
industries within regions according to the 2024 Annual Integrated Economic Survey from the US Census Bureau, highlighting the 
extent to which regional economies are concentrated in particular sectors. 
Although wholesale trade was the sector with the largest share of revenue in each region in the US, there is notable variance in 
economic activity from business revenues across the country. For example, finance and insurance accounted for more than 20% 
of revenue in the Northeast, whereas it’s about half that in the West. Also, manufacturing was only 8.5% of revenue in the 
Northeast, and more than 20% in the Midwest. Overall, the data shows a dynamic national business landscape, with broad 
entrepreneurial activity but distinct regional differences in the industries generating revenue.

Methodology  
Selected Bank of America transaction data is used to inform the macroeconomic views expressed in this report and should be considered in the 
context of other economic indicators and publicly available information. In certain instances, the data may provide directional and/or predictive 
value. The data used is not comprehensive; it is based on aggregated and anonymized selections of Bank of America data and may reflect a 
degree of selection bias and limitations on the data available. 
Any payments data represents aggregated spend from US Retail, Preferred, Small Business and Wealth Management clients with a deposit 
account or credit card. Aggregated spend includes total credit card, debit card, ACH, wires, bill pay, business/peer-to-peer, cash, and checks.  
Any Small Business payments data represents aggregate spend from Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit 
card. Payroll payments data include channels such as ACH (automated clearing house), bill pay, checks and wire. Bank of America per Small 
Business client data represents activity spending from active Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit card and 
at least one transaction in each month. Small businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America and generally defined as 
under $5mm in annual sales revenue.  
Unless otherwise stated, data is not adjusted for seasonality, processing days or portfolio changes, and may be subject to periodic revisions.  
Any household consumer deposit data based on Bank of America internal data is derived by anonymizing and aggregating data from Bank of 
America consumer deposit accounts in the US and analyzing that data at a highly aggregated level. Whenever median household savings and 
checking balances are quoted, the data is based on a fixed cohort of households that had a consumer deposit account (checking and/or savings 
account) for all months from January 2019 through the most current month of data shown.  
Bank of America aggregated credit/debit card spending per household includes spending from active US households only. Only consumer card 
holders making a minimum of five transactions a month are included in the dataset. Spending from corporate cards is excluded. Data regarding 
merchants who receive payments are identified and classified by the Merchant Categorization Code (MCC) defined by financial services 
companies. The data are mapped using proprietary methods from the MCCs to the North American Industry Classification System (NAICS), 
which is also used by the Census Bureau, in order to classify spending data by subsector. Spending data may also be classified by other 
proprietary methods not using MCCs. Value grocers is defined using a combination of MCC code and proprietary methods.  
Lower, middle and higher household income cuts in Bank of America credit and debit card spending per household, and consumer deposit 
account data are based on quantitative estimates of each households’ income. These quantitative estimates are bucketed according to terciles, 
with a third of households placed in each tercile periodically. The lowest tercile represents ‘lower income’, the middle tercile represents ‘middle 
income’ and the highest tercile ‘higher income’. The income thresholds between these terciles will move over time, reflecting any number of 
factors that impact income, including general wage inflation, changes in social security payments and individual households’ income. The 
income and tercile in which a household is categorised are periodically re-assessed.  
Generations, if discussed, are defined as follows: Gen Z, born after 1995; Younger Millennials: born between 1989-1995; Older Millennials: born 
between 1978-1988; Gen Xers: born between 1965-1977; Baby Boomer: 1946-1964; Traditionalists: pre-1946. 
Any reference to card spending per household on gasoline includes all purchases at gasoline stations and might include purchases of non-gas 
items. 
Additional information about the methodology used to aggregate the data is available upon request.

## Document 411: US Equity Views_ Over-earning, but not an earnings bubble

### Page 2

semiconductor share prices have recently lagged the trajectory of near-term EPS
estimates. Similarly, the market trades at a P/E discount relative to what a macro model
would imply given the current level of corporate profitability. Our 12-month S&P 500
return forecast of +14% (to 8,700) reflects the view that earnings growth, rather than
expanding valuations, will remain the primary driver of the bull market.

### Page 3

The magnitude of S&P 500 “over-earning”

S&P 500 earnings grew by an extraordinary 51% year/year in Q2 2026 and have 
grown by 26% during the past four quarters. During the last 30 years, the recent pace 
of earnings growth has been exceeded only during the post-recession rebounds in 2010 
and 2021.

The recent strength of S&P 500 earnings growth has raised investor concerns that 
current earnings power is unsustainable and that the market is in an “earnings 
bubble.” S&P 500 earnings have recently outpaced both their historical trend and their 
historical relationship with US GDP growth. S&P 500 4-quarter EPS growth has averaged 
7% during the past 30 years, and the relationship between S&P 500 earnings and US 
economic growth in recent decades would have pointed to a similar rate of earnings 
growth during the last few quarters.

Exhibit 1: S&P 500 trailing 4-quarter EPS has grown by 26% during the past year

### Page 4

The degree to which the US equity market is currently “expensive” depends in large 
part on the sustainability of recent earnings strength. The exceptional recent 
strength of earnings has driven an unusual wedge between equity valuation multiples 
based on near-term earnings and valuations on trend earnings. Because equity prices 
have failed to keep pace with surging earnings, near-term valuations show no hint of a 
bubble: The S&P 500 12-month P/E ratio has declined from 23x a year ago to 19x today 
and now matches its 10-year average. However, even an “average” multiple may be 
expensive if current earnings are unsustainable. The cyclically-adjusted P/E based on 
trailing 10-year earnings has climbed to one of its highest levels on record, falling shy of 
the highs of 1999-2000 but surpassing the level reached in 2021. Similarly, our 
colleagues have argued that the increase in market value of AI companies during the last 
few years requires an optimistic estimate of the future magnitude of AI-related profits 
and who will capture them.

### Page 5

The AI boom and S&P 500 earnings growth

Affirming investor concerns that US companies are “over-earning,” there are three 
factors that are boosting earnings today but will contribute to deceleration going 
forward: 1) AI capex spending, 2) semiconductor margin expansion, and 3) earnings 
from private investment gains.

AI capex 
The AI investment boom is driving nearly half of S&P 500 EPS growth this year, but 
its contribution will fade going forward. The mega-cap US hyperscalers are on track to 
spend $800 billion on capex this year, an increase of 94% vs. 2025. That spending is 
flowing through to the earnings of the AI infrastructure complex including 
semiconductor, tech hardware, industrials, and utilities companies, which are collectively 
accounting for roughly half of consensus S&P 500 earnings growth this year. The AI 
boom is also having a secondary impact on earnings outside of the infrastructure 
complex, for example by boosting capital markets activity and consumer wealth. Both 
consensus and GS analyst forecasts show hyperscaler capex growing at a slower rate in 
coming years, which should result in decelerating earnings growth for much of the AI 
infrastructure complex and a fading tailwind to S&P 500 earnings.

Hyperscaler depreciation expenses will continue to increase as capex growth 
decelerates, further dampening the boost of AI investment spending to S&P 500 
earnings growth. We estimate a drag from hyperscaler depreciation expenses on S&P 
500 earnings growth of 5pp in 2027, offsetting nearly half of the 11pp boost to earnings 
from capex spending. By 2028, the drag from depreciation should offset the S&P 500 
earnings uplift from continued capex spending.

### Page 7

If capex were fully expensed, consensus 2027 S&P 500 EPS would be 22% lower than 
under current accounting rules. The current gap between actual reported and 
hypothetical fully-expensed earnings is one of the widest in recent decades, exceeded 
only as earnings fell more quickly than capex during the recessions of 2001 and 2008.

Hyperscaler capex has consistently surprised relative to consensus estimates during 
the last few years, and the potential for additional surprises going forward creates a 
wide range of potential S&P 500 earnings outcomes. Our equity analysts expect 
hyperscaler capex will total $1.2 trillion in 2027 and $1.4 trillion in 2028, in which case 
the current tailwind from AI capex to S&P 500 EPS will fade by 2028. Exhibit 10 below 
outlines upside and downside scenarios around that base case. Next year, a $250 billion 
surprise in hyperscaler capex — in either direction — would shift S&P 500 EPS growth by 
about 6 pp in the same direction.  
We estimate that hyperscaler capex would need to decline by roughly 30%, to $570 
billion, to offset earnings growth for the rest of the S&P 500 in 2027. This decline 
would reduce earnings for the AI infrastructure complex by about 40%, leaving their 
profits about 25% above 2025 levels.

### Page 8

Semiconductor margin expansion 
A sharp increase in profit margins for many semiconductor companies has 
contributed to the above-trend recent pace of S&P 500 earnings growth. The 
combination of strong demand and limited supply has boosted semiconductor prices 
and gross margins, with the most pronounced margin expansion among memory stocks. 
S&P 500 memory firms currently enjoy gross margins of roughly 80%, more than double 
their historical average. Across the semiconductor industry, gross margins sit at their 
highest level in decades, and consensus expects margins to remain at similar levels 
through 2028.

Margin expansion has accounted for a large share of recent semiconductor earnings 
growth, but that boost should fade going forward. We estimate that about 1/4 of S&P 
500 Semiconductor earnings growth in 2026 is being driven by gross margin expansion. 
Our equity analysts believe the balance of memory supply and demand is likely to 
remain tight through 2027, with incremental supply additions unlikely to impact industry 
margins until 2028 at the earliest. However, they expect the rate of incremental margin 
expansion to slow meaningfully next year, reducing the contribution of semiconductors
